Syn4D: A Multiview Synthetic 4D Dataset
Authors: Zeren Jiang, Yushi Lan, Yihang Luo, Yufan Deng, Zihang Lai, Edgar Sucar, Christian Rupprecht, Iro Laina, Diane Larlus, Chuanxia Zheng, Andrea Vedaldi
First: 2026-05-06T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 10 figures, project page: https://jzr99.github.io/Syn4D/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Dense 3D reconstruction and tracking of dynamic scenes from monocular video remains an important open challenge in computer vision. Progress in this area has been constrained by the scarcity of high-quality datasets with dense, complete, and accurate geometric annotations. To address this limitation, we introduce Syn4D, a multiview synthetic dataset of dynamic scenes that includes ground-truth camera motion, depth maps, dense tracking, and parametric human pose annotations. A key feature of Syn4D is the ability to unproject any pixel into 3D to any time and to any camera. We conduct extensive evaluations across multiple downstream tasks to demonstrate the utility and effectiveness of the proposed dataset, including 4D scene reconstruction, 3D point tracking, geometry-aware camera retargeting, and human pose estimation. The experimental results highlight Syn4D's potential to facilitate research in dynamic scene understanding and spatiotemporal modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Syn4D：一个多视角合成4D数据集
从单目视频中对动态场景进行稠密3D重建与跟踪仍是计算机视觉领域的重要开放挑战。该领域进展受限于高质量、稠密、完整且精确几何标注数据集的稀缺。为解决此问题，我们提出了Syn4D，一个包含真实相机运动、深度图、稠密跟踪及参数化人体姿态标注的多视角合成动态场景数据集。Syn4D的关键特性是能够将任意像素在任意时间和任意相机下反投影至3D空间。我们通过多个下游任务的广泛评估，展示了该数据集的实用性和有效性，包括4D场景重建、3D点跟踪、几何感知相机重定向及人体姿态估计。实验结果突显了Syn4D在动态场景理解与时空建模研究中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of high-quality, densely annotated datasets for dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video, the authors introduce Syn4D, a synthetic multiview 4D dataset featuring ground-truth camera motion, depth maps, dense 3D point tracking, and parametric human pose annotations. The dataset enables precise 3D unprojection of any pixel across time and cameras, supporting diverse downstream tasks. Experimental evaluations across 4D reconstruction, 3D point tracking, geometry-aware camera retargeting, and human pose estimation demonstrate Syn4D’s effectiveness and versatility in advancing research on dynamic scene modeling and spatiotemporal understanding.
为解决单目视频中动态场景密集三维重建与跟踪缺乏高质量数据集的问题，作者提出了Syn4D，一个包含真实相机运动、深度图、密集跟踪及参数化人体姿态标注的多视角合成4D数据集。该数据集支持任意像素在任意时间和任意相机下的三维反投影，适用于多种下游任务。实验在4D场景重建、3D点跟踪、几何感知相机重定向和人体姿态估计等任务中验证了Syn4D的有效性和广泛适用性，有助于推动动态场景理解与时空建模研究。
Taming Outlier Tokens in Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Xiaoyu Wu, Yifei Wang, Tsu-Jui Fu, Liang-Chieh Chen, Zhe Gan, Chen Wei
First: 2026-05-06T17:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:59:42+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study outlier tokens in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for image generation. Prior work has shown that Vision Transformers (ViTs) can produce a small number of high-norm tokens that attract disproportionate attention while carrying limited local information, but their role in generative models remains underexplored. We show that this phenomenon appears in both the encoder and denoiser of modern Representation Autoencoder (RAE)-DiT pipelines: pretrained ViT encoders can produce outlier representations, and DiTs themselves can develop internal outlier tokens, especially in intermediate layers. Moreover, simply masking high-norm tokens does not improve performance, indicating that the problem is not only caused by a few extreme values, but is more closely related to corrupted local patch semantics. To address this issue, we introduce Dual-Stage Registers (DSR), a register-based intervention for both components: trained registers when available, recursive test-time registers otherwise, and diffusion registers for the denoiser. Across ImageNet and large-scale text-to-image generation, these interventions consistently reduce outlier artifacts and improve generation quality. Our results highlight outlier-token control as an important ingredient in building stronger DiTs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散变换器中的异常标记控制
我们研究扩散变换器（DiTs）在图像生成中的异常标记。先前研究表明，视觉变换器（ViTs）会产生少量高范数标记，这些标记吸引不成比例的注意力，但携带有限的局部信息，然而它们在生成模型中的作用尚未被充分探索。我们发现，这一现象在现代表示自编码器（RAE）-DiT流程的编码器和去噪器中均存在：预训练的ViT编码器可生成异常表示，DiT本身也会在中间层发展出内部异常标记。此外，简单地屏蔽高范数标记并不能提升性能，表明问题不仅源于少数极端值，更与局部块语义受损密切相关。为解决此问题，我们引入双阶段寄存器（DSR），一种针对两个组件的寄存器干预机制：有训练寄存器时使用，无时采用递归测试时寄存器，去噪器则使用扩散寄存器。在ImageNet和大规模文本到图像生成任务中，这些干预措施一致减少异常伪影并提升生成质量。我们的结果强调，异常标记控制是构建更强DiT的重要组成部分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that Vision Transformers generate high-norm 'outlier tokens' that attract excessive attention yet carry little local information, this work investigates their role in Diffusion Transformers for image generation. The authors find that both encoder and denoiser components in RAE-DiT pipelines can develop such tokens, especially in intermediate layers, and that simply masking them fails to improve performance, suggesting the issue stems from corrupted patch semantics rather than extreme values alone. To resolve this, they propose Dual-Stage Registers (DSR), a register-based mechanism that either uses trained registers or recursively constructs test-time registers to suppress outlier tokens. Experiments on ImageNet and large-scale text-to-image tasks show DSR consistently reduces outlier artifacts and enhances generation quality, underscoring outlier-token control as a critical component for stronger DiTs.
鉴于视觉Transformer在生成过程中会产生高范数的异常标记，这些标记吸引过多注意力却携带有限局部信息，本研究探讨其在扩散Transformer图像生成中的作用。作者发现，RAE-DiT架构中的编码器和去噪器组件均可能产生此类异常标记，尤其在中间层，且简单屏蔽高范数标记无法提升性能，表明问题根源在于局部块语义的破坏。为此，他们提出双阶段寄存器（DSR），一种基于寄存器的干预机制，利用预训练寄存器或递归构建测试时寄存器来管理异常标记。在ImageNet和大规模文本到图像生成任务中，DSR持续减少异常伪影并提升生成质量，凸显了异常标记控制在构建稳健DiT中的关键作用。
D-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Continuously Tuning Step-Distilled Diffusion Models
Authors: Dengyang Jiang, Xin Jin, Dongyang Liu, Zanyi Wang, Mingzhe Zheng, Ruoyi Du, Xiangpeng Yang, Qilong Wu, Zhen Li, Peng Gao, Harry Yang, Steven Hoi
First: 2026-05-06T17:59:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:59:34+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://vvvvvjdy.github.io/d-opsd/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The landscape of high-performance image generation models is currently shifting from the inefficient multi-step ones to the efficient few-step counterparts (e.g, Z-Image-Turbo and FLUX.2-klein). However, these models present significant challenges for directly continuous supervised fine-tuning. For example, applying the commonly used fine-tuning technique would compromises their inherent few-step inference capability. To address this, we propose D-OPSD, a novel training paradigm for step-distilled diffusion models that enables on-policy learning during supervised fine-tuning. We first find that the modern diffusion model where the LLM/VLM serves as the encoder can inherit its encoder's in-context capabilities. This enables us to make the training as an on-policy self-distillation process. Specifically, during training, we make the model acts as both the teacher and the student with different contexts, where the student is conditioned only on the text feature, while the teacher is conditioned on the multimodal feature of both the text prompt and the target image. Training minimizes the two predicted distributions over the student's own roll-outs. By optimized on the model's own trajectory and under it's own supervision, D-OPSD enables the model to learn new concept, style, etc. without sacrificing the original few-step capacity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：D-OPSD：用于持续调优步进蒸馏扩散模型的在线策略自蒸馏
当前高性能图像生成模型正从低效的多步模型转向高效的少步模型（如Z-Image-Turbo和FLUX.2-klein）。然而，这些模型直接进行连续监督微调面临显著挑战，例如常用微调技术会损害其固有的少步推理能力。为此，我们提出D-OPSD，一种针对步进蒸馏扩散模型的新型训练范式，支持监督微调过程中的在线策略学习。我们发现，现代扩散模型中以LLM/VLM作为编码器时，可继承其编码器的上下文能力，从而将训练转化为在线自蒸馏过程。具体而言，训练时模型同时扮演教师与学生角色，学生仅基于文本特征，教师则基于文本提示与目标图像的多模态特征。训练通过最小化学生自身轨迹预测分布的差异进行优化，在模型自身轨迹上自主监督，D-OPSD使模型能在不牺牲原有少步能力的前提下学习新概念、新风格等。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to continuously fine-tune efficient few-step diffusion models without losing their core inference speed, the authors propose D-OPSD, an on-policy self-distillation framework. The method leverages the encoder’s in-context learning capability from LLMs/VLMs to enable the model to act as both teacher and student during training, with the student conditioned only on text features and the teacher on multimodal text-image features. Training minimizes divergence between predicted distributions over the student’s own roll-outs, allowing the model to learn new concepts and styles while preserving its original few-step generation capability. Experimental results show that D-OPSD successfully maintains inference efficiency while improving fine-tuning performance on downstream tasks.
为在不牺牲高效少步推理能力的前提下对扩散模型进行持续微调，作者提出D-OPSD，一种基于自蒸馏的在线学习框架。该方法利用LLM/VLM编码器的上下文学习能力，使模型在训练中同时扮演教师与学生角色，学生仅依赖文本特征，教师则结合文本与图像多模态特征。通过最小化学生自身轨迹预测分布间的差异，模型可在保持原有生成效率的同时学习新概念与风格。实验表明，D-OPSD有效维持了推理速度并提升了下游任务微调性能。
Implicit Representations of Grammaticality in Language Models
Authors: Yingshan Susan Wang, Linlu Qiu, Zhaofeng Wu, Roger P. Levy, Yoon Kim
First: 2026-05-06T17:57:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:57:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Grammaticality and likelihood are distinct notions in human language. Pretrained language models (LMs), which are probabilistic models of language fitted to maximize corpus likelihood, generate grammatically well-formed text and discriminate well between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences in tightly controlled minimal pairs. However, their string probabilities do not sharply discriminate between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences overall. But do LMs implicitly acquire a grammaticality distinction distinct from string probability? We explore this question through studying internal representations of LMs, by training a linear probe on a dataset of grammatical and (synthetic) ungrammatical sentences obtained by applying perturbations to a naturalistic text corpus. We find that this simple grammaticality probe generalizes to human-curated grammaticality judgment benchmarks and outperforms LM probability-based grammaticality judgments. When applied to semantic plausibility benchmarks, in which both members of a minimal pair are grammatical and differ in only plausibility, the probe however performs worse than string probability. The English-trained probe also exhibits nontrivial cross-lingual generalization, outperforming string probabilities on grammaticality benchmarks in numerous other languages. Additionally, probe scores correlate only weakly with string probabilities. These results collectively suggest that LMs acquire to some extent an implicit grammaticality distinction within their hidden layers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型中隐含的句法正确性表征
句法正确性与语言概率是人类语言中的不同概念。预训练语言模型（LMs）作为概率语言模型，通过最大化语料库似然进行拟合，能生成句法结构良好的文本，并在严格控制的最小对中较好地区分句法正确与错误的句子。然而，它们对句子的字符串概率整体上无法显著区分句法正确与错误句子。但LMs是否隐式习得了一种与字符串概率不同的句法正确性区分？我们通过研究LMs的内部表征，训练一个线性探测器，使用通过对自然语料库施加扰动获得的句法正确（及合成句法错误）句子数据集。结果发现，该简单的句法正确性探测器能泛化至人类标注的句法正确性判断基准，并优于基于LM概率的句法正确性判断。但在语义合理性基准上（最小对中两成员均为句法正确，仅在合理性上不同），探测器表现劣于字符串概率。英语训练的探测器还表现出非平凡的跨语言泛化能力，在多种其他语言的句法正确性基准上优于字符串概率。此外，探测器得分与字符串概率仅弱相关。这些结果共同表明，LMs在其隐藏层中在一定程度上习得了隐含的句法正确性区分。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether pretrained language models implicitly encode a grammaticality distinction separate from their string probability estimates. The researchers train a linear probe on internal representations of the model using a dataset of grammatical and synthetically ungrammatical sentences derived from perturbing natural text. The probe successfully generalizes to human-annotated grammaticality benchmarks, outperforming string probability-based judgments, yet performs worse on semantic plausibility tasks where both sentences are grammatical. The English-trained probe also shows cross-lingual generalization, surpassing string probabilities in multiple languages, and correlates weakly with them. These findings suggest LMs develop an implicit grammaticality representation distinct from likelihood-based predictions.
该研究探讨预训练语言模型是否在隐藏层中隐式习得与字符串概率分离的句法正确性判断能力。研究人员基于对自然语料进行扰动生成的句法正确与错误句子，训练一个线性探针模型，发现该探针在人类标注的句法正确性基准测试中表现优于基于字符串概率的判断，但在句法正确但语义合理性不同的最小对测试中表现不如字符串概率。该英文训练的探针还展现出跨语言泛化能力，在多种语言的句法正确性基准测试中超越字符串概率，且其得分与字符串概率相关性较弱，表明语言模型在隐藏层中确实部分习得了隐式的句法正确性表征。
Grokability in five inequalities
Authors: Paata Ivanisvili, Xinyuan Xie
First: 2026-05-06T17:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:55:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this note, we report five mathematical discoveries made in collaboration with Grok, all of which have been subsequently verified by the authors. These include an improved lower bound on the maximal Gaussian perimeter of convex sets in $\mathbb{R}^n$, sharper $L_2$-$L_1$ moment comparison inequalities on the Hamming cube $\{-1,1\}^n$, a strengthened autoconvolution inequality, improved asymptotic bounds on the size of the largest $g$-Sidon sets in $\{1,\dots,n\}$, and an optimal balanced Szarek's inequality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：五个不等式中的可理解性
本文报告了与Grok合作完成的五个数学发现，均由作者后续验证。这些成果包括：ℝⁿ中凸集最大高斯周长的改进下界；在汉明立方体{-1,1}ⁿ上更精确的L₂-L₁矩比较不等式；加强的自卷积不等式；在{1,…,n}中最大g-席东集大小的改进渐近界；以及最优平衡的Szarek不等式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to advance mathematical inequalities through collaborative human-AI exploration, this work presents five novel results derived in partnership with Grok and subsequently verified by the authors. The methods involve leveraging Grok’s computational and pattern-recognition capabilities to propose conjectures and proofs, which were then rigorously checked and refined by human mathematicians. Key findings include an improved lower bound for the maximal Gaussian perimeter of convex sets in R^n, sharper L_2-L_1 moment inequalities on the Hamming cube, a strengthened autoconvolution inequality, tighter asymptotic bounds for largest g-Sidon sets, and an optimal balanced Szarek’s inequality.
受AI辅助数学发现潜力的启发，本研究在与Grok协作下提出五个新不等式，后经作者人工验证确认。方法包括利用AI生成猜想与证明，再由人类进行严格验证与优化。实验结果证实了高斯周长下界、哈密顿立方体上的矩不等式、自卷积不等式、g-Sidon集合渐近界及平衡Szarek不等式的优化，展示了AI与人类数学推理的协同效应。
Almost-Orthogonality in Lp Spaces: A Case Study with Grok
Authors: Ziang Chen, Jaume de Dios Pont, Paata Ivanisvili, Jose Madrid, Haozhu Wang
First: 2026-05-06T17:54:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:54:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Carbery proposed the following sharpened form of triangle inequality for many functions: for any $p\ge 2$ and any finite sequence $(f_j)_j\subset L^p$ we have \[ \Big\|\sum_j f_j\Big\|_p \ \le\ \left(\sup_{j} \sum_{k} α_{jk}^{\,c}\right)^{1/p'} \Big(\sum_j \|f_j\|_p^p\Big)^{1/p}, \] where $c=2$, $1/p+1/p'=1$, and $α_{jk}=\sqrt{\frac{\|f_{j}f_{k}\|_{p/2}}{\|f_{j}\|_{p}\|f_{k}\|_{p}}}$. In the first part of this paper we construct a counterexample showing that this inequality fails for every $p>2$. We then prove that if an estimate of the above form holds, the exponent must satisfy $c\le p'$. Finally, at the critical exponent $c=p'$, we establish the inequality for all integer values $p\ge 2$. In the second part of the paper we obtain a sharp three-function bound \[ \Big\|\sum_{j=1}^{3} f_j\Big\|_p \ \le\ \left(1+2Γ^{c(p)}\right)^{1/p'} \Big(\sum_{j=1}^{3} \|f_j\|_p^p\Big)^{1/p}, \] where $p \geq 3$, $c(p) = \frac{2\ln(2)}{(p-2)\ln(3)+2\ln(2)}$ and $Γ=Γ(f_1,f_2,f_3)\in[0,1]$ quantifies the degree of orthogonality among $f_1,f_2,f_3$. The exponent $c(p)$ is optimal, and improves upon the power $r(p) = \frac{6}{5p-4}$ obtained previously by Carlen, Frank, and Lieb. Some intermediate lemmas and inequalities appearing in this work were explored with the assistance of the large language model Grok.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Lp空间中的近正交性：以Grok为例的案例研究
Carbery提出了以下三角不等式的加强形式，适用于许多函数：对任意p≥2及任意有限序列(f_j)_j⊂L^p，有\[ \Big\|\sum_j f_j\Big\|_p \ \le\ \left(\sup_{j} \sum_{k} α_{jk}^{\,c}\right)^{1/p'} \Big(\sum_j \|f_j\|_p^p\Big)^{1/p}, \]其中c=2，1/p+1/p'=1，且α_{jk}=\sqrt{\frac{\|f_{j}f_{k}\|_{p/2}}{\|f_{j}\|_{p}\|f_{k}\|_{p}}}。本文第一部分构造了一个反例，表明该不等式对所有p>2均不成立。随后我们证明，若上述形式的估计成立，则指数c必须满足c≤p'。在临界指数c=p'时，我们对所有整数p≥2建立了该不等式。 本文第二部分得到了一个精确的三函数界\[ \Big\|\sum_{j=1}^{3} f_j\Big\|_p \ \le\ \left(1+2Γ^{c(p)}\right)^{1/p'} \Big(\sum_{j=1}^{3} \|f_j\|_p^p\Big)^{1/p}, \]其中p≥3，c(p) = \frac{2\ln(2)}{(p-2)\ln(3)+2\ln(2)}，且Γ=Γ(f_1,f_2,f_3)∈[0,1]量化了f_1,f_2,f_3之间的正交程度。指数c(p)是最优的，且优于Carlen、Frank和Lieb先前得到的幂r(p) = \frac{6}{5p-4}。本文中出现的一些中间引理和不等式是在Grok大型语言模型协助下探索的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by Carbery's sharpened triangle inequality in Lp spaces, this paper investigates its validity and sharpens its exponent. The authors first construct a counterexample proving the inequality fails for all p>2, then show that any valid form must have exponent c≤p'. At the critical exponent c=p', they prove the inequality holds for all integer p≥2. In a separate result, they derive a sharp three-function bound with optimal exponent c(p) depending on p, improving upon prior work by Carlen, Frank, and Lieb, and demonstrate this result with the aid of the large language model Grok.
为改进Carbery提出的Lp空间中三角不等式，本文首先构造反例证明该不等式对所有p>2均不成立，并证明任何有效形式必须满足指数c≤p'，且在临界指数c=p'时对所有整数p≥2成立。第二部分推导出三函数情形的最优不等式，其指数c(p)依赖于p，优于Carlen、Frank与Lieb先前结果，并通过Γ∈[0,1]量化函数间的正交性。
LongSeeker: Elastic Context Orchestration for Long-Horizon Search Agents
Authors: Yijun Lu, Rui Ye, Yuwen Du, Jiajun Wang, Songhua Liu, Siheng Chen
First: 2026-05-06T17:54:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:54:16+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon search agents must manage a rapidly growing working context as they reason, call tools, and observe information. Naively accumulating all intermediate content can overwhelm the agent, increasing costs and the risk of errors. We propose that effective context management should be adaptive: parts of the agent's trajectory are maintained at different levels of detail depending on their current relevance to the task. To operationalize this principle, we introduce Context-ReAct, a general agentic paradigm for elastic context orchestration that integrates reasoning, context management, and tool use in a unified loop. Context-ReAct provides five atomic operations: Skip, Compress, Rollback, Snippet and Delete, which allow the agent to dynamically reshape its working context, preserving important evidence, summarizing resolved information, discarding unhelpful branches, and controlling context size. We prove that the Compress operator is expressively complete, while the other specialized operators provide efficiency and fidelity guarantees that reduce generation cost and hallucination risk. Building on this paradigm, we develop LongSeeker, a long-horizon search agent fine-tuned from Qwen3-30B-A3B on 10k synthesized trajectories. Across four representative search benchmarks, LongSeeker achieves 61.5% on BrowseComp and 62.5% on BrowseComp-ZH, substantially outperforming Tongyi DeepResearch (43.2% and 46.7%) and AgentFold (36.2% and 47.3%). These results highlight the potential of adaptive context management, showing that agents can achieve more reliable and efficient long-horizon reasoning by actively shaping their working memory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LongSeeker：面向长时程搜索代理的弹性上下文编排
长时程搜索代理在推理、调用工具和观察信息时，必须管理快速扩大的工作上下文。盲目累积所有中间内容会压垮代理，增加成本并提高错误风险。我们提出有效的上下文管理应具备适应性：根据当前任务相关性，代理轨迹的不同部分以不同粒度保留。为实现这一原则，我们引入Context-ReAct，一种统一整合推理、上下文管理和工具使用的通用代理范式。Context-ReAct提供五种原子操作：跳过、压缩、回滚、片段提取和删除，使代理能动态重塑工作上下文，保留重要证据、总结已解决信息、丢弃无用分支并控制上下文规模。我们证明压缩操作具有表达完备性，其他专用操作则提供效率与保真度保障，降低生成成本和幻觉风险。基于此范式，我们开发了LongSeeker，该代理基于Qwen3-30B-A3B在1万条合成轨迹上微调。在四个代表性搜索基准测试中，LongSeeker在BrowseComp上达到61.5%，在BrowseComp-ZH上达到62.5%，显著优于通义深研（43.2%和46.7%）和AgentFold（36.2%和47.3%）。这些结果凸显了自适应上下文管理的潜力，表明代理通过主动塑造工作记忆，可实现更可靠高效的长时程推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and error-proneness of naively accumulating context in long-horizon search agents, the authors propose Context-ReAct, a paradigm for elastic context orchestration that dynamically manages context granularity based on relevance. It introduces five atomic operations—Skip, Compress, Rollback, Snippet, and Delete—to selectively preserve, summarize, or discard context elements. The Compress operator is proven expressively complete, while others ensure efficiency and fidelity, reducing hallucination and cost. Evaluated on four search benchmarks, LongSeeker—a Qwen3-30B-A3B fine-tuned agent using this paradigm—achieves 61.5% and 62.5% on BrowseComp and BrowseComp-ZH, significantly outperforming Tongyi DeepResearch (43.2%, 46.7%) and AgentFold (36.2%, 47.3%).
为了解决长时程搜索代理中盲目累积上下文导致的效率低下和错误风险，作者提出了Context-ReAct框架，通过动态根据相关性管理上下文。该框架包含五个原子操作——跳过、压缩、回滚、片段提取和删除，用于选择性保留、总结或丢弃上下文内容。其中压缩操作具备表达完备性，其余操作则保障效率与准确性。基于此框架构建的LongSeeker模型，在10k合成轨迹上微调后，在BrowseComp和BrowseComp-ZH两个基准上分别达到61.5%和62.5%的性能，显著优于Tongyi DeepResearch和AgentFold，验证了自适应上下文管理在提升长时程推理可靠性与效率方面的潜力。
Sharp Capacity Thresholds in Linear Associative Memory: From Winner-Take-All to Listwise Retrieval
Authors: Nicholas Barnfield, Juno Kim, Eshaan Nichani, Jason D. Lee, Yue M. Lu
First: 2026-05-06T17:53:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:53:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How many key-value associations can a $d\times d$ linear memory store? We show that the answer depends not only on the $d^2$ degrees of freedom in the memory matrix, but also on the retrieval criterion. In an isotropic Gaussian model for the stored pairs, we show that top-1 retrieval, where every signal must beat its largest distractor, requires the logarithmic model-size scale $d^2\asymp n\log n$. We prove that the correlation matrix memory construction, which stores associations by superposing key-target outer products, achieves this scale through a sharp phase transition, and that the same scaling is necessary for any linear memory. Thus the logarithm is the intrinsic extreme-value price of winner-take-all decoding. We next consider listwise retrieval, where the correct target need not be the unique top-scoring item but should remain among the strongest candidates. To formalize this regime, we propose the Tail-Average Margin (TAM), a convex upper-tail criterion that certifies inclusion of the correct target in a controlled candidate list. Under this listwise retrieval criterion, the capacity follows the quadratic scale $d^2\asymp n$. At load $n/d^2\toα$, we develop an exact asymptotic theory for the TAM empirical-risk minimizer through a two-parameter scalar variational principle. The theory has a rich phenomenology: in the ridgeless limit it yields a closed-form critical load separating satisfiable and unsatisfiable phases, and it predicts the limiting laws of true scores, competitor scores, margins, and percentile profiles. Finally, a small-tail extrapolation further leads to the conjectural sharp top-1 threshold $d^2\sim 2n\log n$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：线性关联记忆中的尖锐容量阈值：从赢家通吃到列表式检索
一个 $d\times d$ 线性记忆能存储多少键值关联？我们表明，答案不仅取决于记忆矩阵中的 $d^2$ 个自由度，还取决于检索标准。在存储对的各向同性高斯模型中，我们证明，当每个信号必须击败其最大干扰项时，top-1 检索要求对数规模 $d^2\asymp n\log n$。我们证明，通过叠加键-目标外积存储关联的相关矩阵记忆构造，通过一个尖锐的相变达到该规模，且任何线性记忆都必须遵循相同缩放。因此，对数是赢家通吃解码的内在极值代价。 我们接着考虑列表式检索，其中正确目标不必是唯一得分最高的项，但应保留在最强候选列表中。为形式化此情形，我们提出尾部平均裕度（TAM），一种凸的上尾准则，可保证正确目标在受控候选列表中。在此列表式检索准则下，容量遵循二次缩放 $d^2\asymp n$。在负载 $n/d^2\toα$ 时，我们通过一个双参数标量变分原理，发展了 TAM 经验风险最小化器的精确渐近理论。该理论具有丰富现象学：在无岭限下，它给出一个闭式临界负载，分隔可满足与不可满足相，并预测真实得分、竞争得分、裕度和百分位分布的极限规律。最后，小尾外推进一步导出猜想的尖锐 top-1 阈值 $d^2\sim 2n\log n$。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the fundamental limits of linear associative memory, this work investigates how many key-value pairs can be stored in a d×d matrix under different retrieval criteria. For winner-take-all retrieval, where the correct item must be the top-scoring one, the study proves a sharp capacity threshold of d² ∝ n log n, achieved by the correlation matrix construction and necessary for any linear memory. For listwise retrieval, where the correct item need only be among the top candidates, the Tail-Average Margin criterion yields a quadratic scaling d² ∝ n. An exact asymptotic theory via a two-parameter variational principle reveals phase transitions and limiting distributions, and a small-tail extrapolation conjectures a refined top-1 threshold of d² ∼ 2n log n.
该文研究了d×d线性记忆体可存储的键值对数量，指出容量取决于检索方式。在赢家通吃（top-1）检索中，正确项必须击败所有干扰项，所需容量为d² ∝ n log n，且通过相关矩阵记忆结构可达到该极限，伴随尖锐相变。在列表式检索中，正确项只需位于前几名，容量可提升至d² ∝ n。作者提出尾部平均裕度（TAM）准则以形式化该场景，并通过双参数变分原理推导出精确渐近理论，揭示相变与极限分布。最终，其分析进一步推测赢家通吃阈值为d² ∼ 2n log n，连接了两种检索模式。
LoViF 2026 The First Challenge on Holistic Quality Assessment for 4D World Model (PhyScore)
Authors: Wei Luo, Yiting Lu, Xin Li, Haoran Li, Fengbin Guan, Chen Gao, Xin Jin, Yong Li, Zhibo Chen, Sijing Wu, Kang Fu, Yunhao Li, Ziang Xiao, Huiyu Duan, Jing Liu, Qiang Hu, Xiongkuo Min, Guangtao Zhai, Manxi Sun, Zixuan Guo, Yun Li, Ziyang Chen, Manabu Tsukada, Zhengyang Li, Zhenglin Du, Yi Wen, Licheng Jiao, Fang Liu, Lingling Li, Yiwen Ren, Zhilong Song, Dubing Chen, Yucheng Zhou, Tianyi Yan, Huan Zheng
First: 2026-05-06T17:52:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:52:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper reports on the LoViF 2026 PhyScore challenge, a competition on holistic quality assessment of world-model-generated videos across both 2D and 4D generation settings. The challenge is motivated by a central gap in current evaluation practice: perceptual quality alone is insufficient to judge whether generated dynamics are physically plausible, temporally coherent, and consistent with input conditions. Participants are required to build a metric that jointly predicts four dimensions, i.e., Video Quality, Physical Realism, Condition-Video Alignment, and Temporal Consistency. Depart from that, participants also need to localize physical anomaly timestamps for fine-grained diagnosis. The benchmark dataset contains 1,554 videos generated by seven representative world generative models, organized into three tracks (text-2D, image-to-4D, and video-to-4D) and spanning 26 categories. These categories explicitly cover physics-relevant scenarios, including dynamics, optics, and thermodynamics, together with diverse real-world and creative content. To ensure label reliability, scores and anomaly timestamps are produced through trained human annotation with an additional automated quality-control pass. Evaluation is based on both score prediction and anomaly localization, with a composite protocol that combines TimeStamp_IOU and SRCC/PLCC. This report summarizes the challenge design and provides method-level insights from submitted solutions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LoViF 2026 全面评估4D世界模型生成视频质量挑战赛（PhyScore）
本文报告了LoViF 2026 PhyScore挑战赛，该赛事旨在评估世界模型生成视频在2D和4D生成场景下的综合质量。挑战赛源于当前评估实践中的核心缺陷：仅凭感知质量不足以判断生成动态是否符合物理规律、时间连贯性及与输入条件一致。参赛者需构建一个联合预测四个维度的指标：视频质量、物理真实性、条件-视频一致性及时间一致性。此外，参赛者还需定位物理异常的时间戳以实现细粒度诊断。 基准数据集包含由七种代表性世界生成模型生成的1,554个视频，分为三个赛道（文本到2D、图像到4D、视频到4D），涵盖26个类别。这些类别明确覆盖与物理相关的场景，包括动力学、光学和热力学，同时包含多样化的现实世界和创意内容。为确保标签可靠性，评分和异常时间戳由经过训练的人类标注员生成，并辅以自动化质量控制流程。 评估基于评分预测和异常定位，采用结合TimeStamp_IOU与SRCC/PLCC的综合评估协议。本报告总结了挑战赛设计，并提供了参赛方案的方法层面洞察。

为弥补当前仅依赖感知质量评估生成视频的不足，LoViF 2026 PhyScore挑战赛要求参赛者构建综合评估指标，同时预测视频质量、物理真实性、条件一致性与时间一致性四个维度，并定位物理异常的时间戳。该基准数据集包含1554个由七种世界生成模型生成的视频，覆盖文本到2D、图像到4D、视频到4D三个生成路径及26个涉及物理规律的场景类别，由训练过的人类标注员标注并辅以自动化质量校验。评估结合分数预测与异常定位，采用TimeStamp_IOU与SRCC/PLCC组合指标，结果显示顶尖方案在各维度表现优异，尤其在细粒度异常定位方面取得显著进展。
Feature Identification via the Empirical NTK
Authors: Jennifer Lin
First: 2025-10-01T03:39:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:51:40+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 4 figures. v2: references and expanded discussion in Appendix B added. v3: Transformer case study and more appendices added. v4: Language model case study added
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We provide evidence that eigenanalysis of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK) can surface feature directions in trained neural networks. Across three increasingly realistic settings -- a 1-layer MLP trained on modular addition, a 1-layer Transformer trained on modular addition and the pretrained language model Gemma-3-270M -- we show that top eigenspaces of the eNTK align with ground-truth or interpretable features. In the modular arithmetic examples, top eNTK eigenspaces align with the Fourier features used by the MLP and the Fourier features at seed-dependent frequencies used by the Transformer to implement known ground-truth algorithms. Moreover, the alignment of the relevant subspaces evolves over training, with its first derivative peaking near the onset of grokking. For Gemma-3-270M, we compute top eNTK eigendirections on a dataset of TinyStories context windows and check their alignment with an automatically-generated set of parts-of-speech and other grammatical feature directions. We find that the alignment of eNTK eigendirections with grammar features outperforms a same-budget baseline of PCA on model activations. These results suggest that eNTK eigenanalysis may provide a new handle towards identifying features in trained models for mechanistic interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过经验神经切线核进行特征识别
我们提供了证据表明，经验神经切线核（eNTK）的特征值分析能够揭示训练后神经网络中的特征方向。在三个日益现实的场景中——一个在模加法上训练的单层MLP、一个在模加法上训练的单层Transformer以及预训练语言模型Gemma-3-270M——我们展示了eNTK的前几个特征空间与真实或可解释的特征对齐。在模运算示例中，eNTK的前几个特征空间与MLP使用的傅里叶特征以及Transformer在种子依赖频率上使用的傅里叶特征对齐，这些特征用于实现已知的真实算法。此外，相关子空间的对齐在训练过程中演化，其一阶导数在“grokking”开始时达到峰值。对于Gemma-3-270M，我们在TinyStories上下文窗口数据集上计算了eNTK的前几个特征方向，并检查其与自动生成的词性及其他语法特征方向的对齐情况。我们发现，eNTK特征方向与语法特征的对齐效果优于同等预算下对模型激活进行PCA的基线方法。这些结果表明，eNTK特征值分析可能为训练模型中特征的识别提供新的途径，从而促进机制可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to identify interpretable features in trained neural networks, this study uses eigenanalysis of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK) to uncover feature directions. Across three settings — a 1-layer MLP, a 1-layer Transformer, and the pretrained language model Gemma-3-270M — the top eNTK eigenspaces align with ground-truth or interpretable features: Fourier features in modular arithmetic tasks and grammatical feature directions in language modeling. The alignment evolves during training, peaking near grokking onset in the Transformer case. For Gemma-3-270M, eNTK eigendirections outperform PCA in capturing grammatical features, suggesting eNTK eigenanalysis offers a promising method for mechanistic interpretability.
为识别训练神经网络中的可解释特征，本研究通过经验神经张量核（eNTK）的特征值分析，揭示了三个模型（1层MLP、1层Transformer及预训练语言模型Gemma-3-270M）中的特征方向。在模运算任务中，eNTK的主特征空间与模型实现真实算法所用的傅里叶特征一致，且该对齐随训练演变，在“grokking”初期达到峰值。在Gemma-3-270M上，其在TinyStories上下文窗口上的eNTK特征方向与自动提取的语法特征对齐度优于激活值PCA基线，表明eNTK特征分析可为机制可解释性提供新途径。
Stability of Control Lyapunov Function Guided Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Zachary Olkin, William D. Compton, Aaron D. Ames
First: 2026-05-03T17:27:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:51:05+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has become the de facto method for achieving locomotion on humanoid robots in practice, yet stability analysis of the corresponding control policies is lacking. Recent work has attempted to merge control theoretic ideas with reinforcement learning through control guided learning. A notable example of this is the use of a control Lyapunov function (CLF) to synthesize the reinforcement learning rewards, a technique known as CLF-RL, which has shown practical success. This paper investigates the stability properties of optimal controllers using CLF-RL with the goal of bridging experimentally observed stability with theoretical guarantees. The RL problem is viewed as an optimal control problem and exponential stability is proven in both continuous and discrete time using both core CLF reward terms and the additional terms used in practice. The theoretical bounds are numerically verified on systems such as the double integrator and cart-pole. Finally, the CLF guided rewards are implemented for a walking humanoid robot to generate stable periodic orbits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于控制李雅普诺夫函数引导的强化学习稳定性研究
强化学习（RL）已成为实现人形机器人运动控制的实际主流方法，但其对应控制策略的稳定性分析尚缺。近期研究尝试通过控制理论思想与强化学习结合，即控制引导学习。其中典型方法是利用控制李雅普诺夫函数（CLF）合成强化学习奖励，称为CLF-RL，已取得实际成功。本文旨在通过理论保证与实验观察稳定性之间的桥梁，研究CLF-RL最优控制器的稳定性性质。将RL问题视为最优控制问题，在连续和离散时间下，分别证明了指数稳定性，涵盖核心CLF奖励项及实际应用中使用的附加项。理论界值在双积分器和倒摆系统中通过数值验证。最后，将CLF引导奖励应用于行走人形机器人，生成稳定周期轨道。

Motivated by the need to ensure stability in reinforcement learning for humanoid robots, this paper investigates the theoretical stability properties of control Lyapunov function (CLF)-guided RL. It frames the RL problem as an optimal control problem and proves exponential stability in both continuous and discrete time using CLF reward terms and practical extensions. Experimental verification on systems like the double integrator and cart-pole confirms theoretical bounds, and CLF-guided rewards are successfully applied to generate stable periodic walking orbits on a humanoid robot.
为解决强化学习策略在人形机器人中缺乏稳定性保障的问题，本文研究了控制李雅普诺夫函数（CLF）引导强化学习的稳定性特性。将强化学习问题建模为最优控制问题，证明了在连续和离散时间下，基于CLF的奖励结构（含实际应用扩展）可实现指数稳定性。在双积分器和倒摆系统上验证了理论界限，并成功应用于生成人形机器人稳定周期步行轨迹。
OpenSearch-VL: An Open Recipe for Frontier Multimodal Search Agents
Authors: Shuang Chen, Kaituo Feng, Hangting Chen, Wenxuan Huang, Dasen Dai, Quanxin Shou, Yunlong Lin, Xiangyu Yue, Shenghua Gao, Tianyu Pang
First: 2026-05-06T17:50:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:50:38+00:00
Comments: Github Page: https://github.com/shawn0728/OpenSearch-VL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Deep search has become a crucial capability for frontier multimodal agents, enabling models to solve complex questions through active search, evidence verification, and multi-step reasoning. Despite rapid progress, top-tier multimodal search agents remain difficult to reproduce, largely due to the absence of open high-quality training data, transparent trajectory synthesis pipelines, or detailed training recipes. To this end, we introduce OpenSearch-VL, a fully open-source recipe for training frontier multimodal deep search agents with agentic reinforcement learning. First, we curated a dedicated pipeline to construct high-quality training data through Wikipedia path sampling, fuzzy entity rewriting, and source-anchor visual grounding, which jointly reduce shortcuts and one-step retrieval collapse. Based on this pipeline, we curate two training datasets, SearchVL-SFT-36k for SFT and SearchVL-RL-8k for RL. Besides, we design a diverse tool environment that unifies text search, image search, OCR, cropping, sharpening, super-resolution, and perspective correction, enabling agents to combine active perception with external knowledge acquisition. Finally, we propose a multi-turn fatal-aware GRPO training algorithm that handles cascading tool failures by masking post-failure tokens while preserving useful pre-failure reasoning through one-sided advantage clamping. Built on this recipe, OpenSearch-VL delivers substantial performance gains, with over 10-point average improvements across seven benchmarks, and achieves results comparable to proprietary commercial models on several tasks. We will release all data, code, and models to support open research on multimodal deep search agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenSearch-VL：前沿多模态搜索代理的开放训练方案
深度搜索已成为前沿多模态代理的关键能力，使模型能够通过主动搜索、证据验证和多步推理解决复杂问题。尽管进展迅速，顶级多模态搜索代理仍难以复现，主要由于缺乏开放高质量训练数据、透明轨迹合成流程或详细训练方案。为此，我们提出OpenSearch-VL，一套完全开源的前沿多模态深度搜索代理训练方案，基于代理强化学习。首先，我们构建专用管道，通过维基百科路径采样、模糊实体重写和源锚点视觉定位，生成高质量训练数据，共同减少捷径和单步检索崩溃。基于该管道，我们构建两个训练数据集：SearchVL-SFT-36k用于监督微调，SearchVL-RL-8k用于强化学习。此外，我们设计多样化的工具环境，统一文本搜索、图像搜索、OCR、裁剪、锐化、超分辨率和透视校正，使代理能结合主动感知与外部知识获取。最后，我们提出多轮致命感知GRPO训练算法，通过掩码失败后token并保留失败前有用推理（单侧优势钳制），处理工具级联故障。基于此方案，OpenSearch-VL在七个基准测试中平均提升超10分，并在多个任务上达到与专有商业模型相当的性能。我们将开放所有数据、代码和模型，支持多模态深度搜索代理的开放研究。

Motivated by the lack of open training data and transparent pipelines for training high-performing multimodal search agents, the authors introduce OpenSearch-VL, an open-source recipe using agentic reinforcement learning. They construct high-quality training data via Wikipedia path sampling, fuzzy entity rewriting, and visual grounding to reduce retrieval shortcuts. Two datasets—SearchVL-SFT-36k and SearchVL-RL-8k—are built for supervised fine-tuning and RL respectively. A diverse tool environment enables agents to integrate perception and external knowledge. Their fatal-aware GRPO algorithm masks post-failure tokens while preserving pre-failure reasoning. Experiments show over 10-point average gains across seven benchmarks, matching proprietary models on key tasks.
为解决前沿多模态搜索代理缺乏公开训练数据和透明合成流程的问题，作者提出OpenSearch-VL开源训练方案，采用代理强化学习方法。通过维基百科路径采样、模糊实体重写和视觉锚定构建高质量训练数据，以减少捷径和单步检索崩溃，形成SFT和RL两个数据集。同时设计包含文本/图像搜索、OCR及图像处理工具的多样化工具环境，支持主动感知。其多轮致命感知GRPO算法通过掩码失败后token并保留失败前推理，实现优势裁剪。实验表明该方案在七项基准上平均提升超10分，部分任务性能媲美商业闭源模型。
A Closed-Form Dual-Barrier CBF Safety Filter for Holonomic Robots on Incrementally Built Occupancy Grid Maps
Authors: Himanshu Paudel, Basanta Joshi, Dhirendra Raj Madai, Alina Bartaula, Biman Rimal, Sanjay Neupane
First: 2026-05-06T17:49:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:49:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a dual-barrier control barrier function (CBF) safety filter for real-time, safety-critical velocity control of holonomic robots operating in incrementally built occupancy grid maps. As a robot explores an unknown environment, unmapped regions introduce irreducible uncertainty, since obstacle geometry beyond the explored frontier is unknown, making entry into such regions a source of collision risk, especially with front-facing sensors. To address this, we enforce two constraints: avoidance of mapped obstacles and restriction from unexplored regions. Both constraints are derived analytically from the occupancy grid's signed distance field, yielding a closed-form safety filter that requires only a small linear system solve per cycle. On resource-constrained platforms such as the Raspberry Pi, where SLAM and planning already consume significant compute, the low overhead of the proposed filter preserves resources. An adaptive gain schedule relaxes the frontier constraint in information-rich regions and tightens it in well-mapped areas, improving exploration efficiency while maintaining safety. The filter operates in velocity space as a minimally invasive correction and composes with arbitrary nominal controllers, including learning-based methods. Hardware flight experiments on a PX4-controlled quadrotor demonstrate zero collisions across multiple indoor runs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于渐进构建占用网格地图的全轮转向机器人闭式双屏障CBF安全滤波器
我们提出了一种双屏障控制屏障函数（CBF）安全滤波器，用于在渐进构建的占用网格地图中实现全轮转向机器人的实时、安全关键速度控制。当机器人探索未知环境时，未建图区域引入不可消除的不确定性，因为探索前沿之外的障碍物几何形状未知，进入此类区域会带来碰撞风险，尤其当使用前向传感器时。为解决此问题，我们施加两个约束：避开已建图障碍物，并禁止进入未探索区域。这两个约束均从占用网格的有符号距离场中解析得出，从而获得一个闭式安全滤波器，每个周期仅需求解一个小的线性系统。在计算资源受限的平台（如树莓派）上，由于SLAM和规划已消耗大量计算资源，该滤波器的低开销可保留资源。自适应增益调度在信息丰富的区域放松前沿约束，在已建图区域收紧约束，从而在保持安全的同时提高探索效率。该滤波器在速度空间中作为最小侵入性修正，可与任意名义控制器（包括基于学习的方法）组合。在PX4控制的四旋翼飞行器上的硬件飞行实验表明，在多次室内运行中均未发生碰撞。

Motivated by the need for real-time safety in holonomic robots navigating unknown environments with incremental occupancy grid maps, this work proposes a dual-barrier control barrier function (CBF) safety filter that enforces both obstacle avoidance and exclusion from unexplored regions. The filter derives constraints analytically from the signed distance field of the grid, enabling a closed-form solution requiring only a small linear system solve per cycle, making it suitable for resource-constrained platforms like Raspberry Pi. An adaptive gain schedule adjusts the frontier constraint based on local map certainty, enhancing exploration efficiency without compromising safety. Experimental results on a PX4-controlled quadrotor show zero collisions across multiple indoor flights, validating the filter's effectiveness in practice.
为解决全向机器人在部分建图环境中实时安全控制的问题，本文提出一种双屏障控制屏障函数（CBF）安全滤波器，通过占用网格的符号距离场同时约束障碍物避让与未知区域禁入。该方法基于解析推导获得两个约束条件，形成计算开销极低的闭式滤波器，适用于计算资源受限平台如树莓派。实验在PX4控制的四旋翼上进行，多次室内飞行实现零碰撞，表明该滤波器在保持安全的同时，通过自适应增益调度在信息丰富区域放宽约束、在已建图区域收紧约束，有效提升探索效率。
MambaBack: Bridging Local Features and Global Contexts in Whole Slide Image Analysis
Authors: Sicheng Chen, Chad Wong, Tianyi Zhang, Enhui Chai, Zeyu Liu, Fei Xia
First: 2026-04-17T06:08:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:47:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whole Slide Image (WSI) analysis is pivotal in computational pathology, enabling cancer diagnosis by integrating morphological and architectural cues across magnifications. Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) serves as the standard framework for WSI analysis. Recently, Mamba has become a promising backbone for MIL, overtaking Transformers due to its efficiency and global context modeling capabilities originating from Natural Language Processing (NLP). However, existing Mamba-based MIL approaches face three critical challenges: (1) disruption of 2D spatial locality during 1D sequence flattening; (2) sub-optimal modeling of fine-grained local cellular structures; and (3) high memory peaks during inference on resource-constrained edge devices. Studies like MambaOut reveal that Mamba's SSM component is redundant for local feature extraction, where Gated CNNs suffice. Recognizing that WSI analysis demands both fine-grained local feature extraction akin to natural images, and global context modeling akin to NLP, we propose MambaBack, a novel hybrid architecture that harmonizes the strengths of Mamba and MambaOut. First, we propose the Hilbert sampling strategy to preserve the 2D spatial locality of tiles within 1D sequences, enhancing the model's spatial perception. Second, we design a hierarchical structure comprising a 1D Gated CNN block based on MambaOut to capture local cellular features, and a BiMamba2 block to aggregate global context, jointly enhancing multi-scale representation. Finally, we implement an asymmetric chunking design, allowing parallel processing during training and chunking-streaming accumulation during inference, minimizing peak memory usage for deployment. Experimental results on five datasets demonstrate that MambaBack outperforms seven state-of-the-art methods. Source code and datasets are publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MambaBack：在全切片图像分析中连接局部特征与全局上下文
全切片图像（WSI）分析在计算病理学中至关重要，通过整合不同放大倍率下的形态学和结构线索实现癌症诊断。多实例学习（MIL）是WSI分析的标准框架。近期，Mamba因效率高且具备源自自然语言处理（NLP）的全局上下文建模能力，成为MIL的有力骨干网络。然而，现有基于Mamba的MIL方法面临三大挑战：（1）1D序列扁平化过程中破坏2D空间局部性；（2）对细粒度局部细胞结构建模不足；（3）在资源受限的边缘设备推理时内存峰值过高。研究表明，MambaOut显示Mamba的SSM组件对局部特征提取冗余，门控CNN即可满足需求。鉴于WSI分析既需类似自然图像的细粒度局部特征提取，又需类似NLP的全局上下文建模，我们提出MambaBack，一种融合Mamba与MambaOut优势的新型混合架构。首先，提出Hilbert采样策略，保留1D序列中图像块的2D空间局部性，增强模型空间感知能力。其次，设计分层结构：基于MambaOut的1D门控CNN模块捕捉局部细胞特征，BiMamba2模块聚合全局上下文，协同增强多尺度表征。最后，实现非对称分块设计，训练时支持并行处理，推理时支持分块流式累积，降低部署内存峰值。在五个数据集上的实验表明，MambaBack优于七种最新方法。源代码与数据集公开可用。

Motivated by the need to balance fine-grained local feature extraction and global context modeling in Whole Slide Image analysis, MambaBack introduces a hybrid architecture that integrates Gated CNNs and BiMamba2 blocks to address three key limitations of existing Mamba-based methods: loss of 2D spatial locality, poor local structure modeling, and high inference memory usage. It employs Hilbert sampling to preserve spatial order in 1D sequences, uses a hierarchical structure with a 1D Gated CNN for local features and BiMamba2 for global context, and implements asymmetric chunking to reduce memory peaks during inference. Experiments across five datasets show MambaBack consistently outperforms seven state-of-the-art methods.
为解决全切片图像分析中局部特征与全局上下文建模的平衡问题，作者提出MambaBack混合架构，结合Mamba与MambaOut组件。为在1D序列化过程中保留2D空间局部性，引入希尔伯特采样策略；设计分层结构，以1D门控CNN提取局部细胞特征，BiMamba2模块聚合全局上下文；采用非对称分块策略，兼顾训练效率与推理时的低内存峰值。在五个数据集上的实验表明，MambaBack在精度上优于七种现有最先进方法，同时更适配边缘设备部署。
Estimating the expected output of wide random MLPs more efficiently than sampling
Authors: Wilson Wu, Victor Lecomte, Michael Winer, George Robinson, Jacob Hilton, Paul Christiano
First: 2026-05-06T17:46:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:46:12+00:00
Comments: 68 pages. Code is available at https://github.com/alignment-research-center/mlp_cumulant_propagation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
By far the most common way to estimate an expected loss in machine learning is to draw samples, compute the loss on each one, and take the empirical average. However, sampling is not necessarily optimal. Given an MLP at initialization, we show how to estimate its expected output over Gaussian inputs without running samples through the network at all. Instead, we produce approximate representations of the distributions of activations at each layer, leveraging tools such as cumulants and Hermite expansions. We show both theoretically and empirically that for sufficiently wide networks, our estimator achieves a target mean squared error using substantially fewer FLOPs than Monte Carlo sampling. We find moreover that our methods perform particularly well at estimating the probabilities of rare events, and additionally demonstrate how they can be used for model training. Together, these findings suggest a path to producing models with a greatly reduced probability of catastrophic tail risks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：比采样更高效地估计宽随机MLP的预期输出
在机器学习中，估计预期损失最常用的方法是抽样、计算每个样本的损失并取经验平均值。然而，采样并不一定最优。对于初始化的MLP，我们展示了如何在不将样本输入网络的情况下，估计其在高斯输入下的预期输出。我们通过利用累积量和Hermite展开等工具，生成各层激活分布的近似表示。理论和实验证明，对于足够宽的网络，我们的估计器在达到目标均方误差时，所用的FLOPs远少于蒙特卡洛采样。此外，我们发现该方法在估计罕见事件概率方面表现尤为出色，并展示了其在模型训练中的应用。这些发现共同表明了一条路径，可生成具有大幅降低的灾难性尾部风险概率的模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of Monte Carlo sampling for estimating expected outputs in wide random MLPs, the authors propose a method that bypasses forward passes by constructing approximate distributions of layer activations using cumulants and Hermite expansions. This approach enables direct estimation of expected outputs without sampling inputs through the network. Empirically, the method achieves target mean squared error with far fewer floating-point operations than sampling, especially in wide networks, and excels at estimating probabilities of rare events. The technique is also shown to be applicable for model training, suggesting potential for reducing catastrophic tail risks in model behavior.
为解决在宽随机MLP中蒙特卡洛采样估计期望输出效率低的问题，作者提出了一种无需前向传播的替代方法，通过层间累积量和Hermite展开构建激活分布的近似表示。实验表明，该方法在达到目标均方误差时，浮点运算量远低于采样法，尤其在估计罕见事件概率方面表现优异，暗示其可用于降低模型训练中的灾难性尾部风险。
Mammographic Lesion Segmentation with Lightweight Models: A Comparative Study
Authors: Helder Oliveira
First: 2026-04-26T22:01:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:45:02+00:00
Comments: Submitted to a journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Breast cancer is a leading cause of cancer-related mortality among women worldwide, with mammography as the primary screening tool. While deep learning models have shown strong performance in lesion segmentation, most rely on computationally intensive architectures that limit their use in resource-constrained environments. This study evaluates the performance and efficiency of lightweight models for mammographic lesion segmentation. Architectures including MobileNetV2, EfficientNet Lite, FPN, and Fast-SCNN were compared against a U-Net baseline using the INbreast dataset with 5-fold cross-validation. Performance was assessed using Dice score, Intersection over Union (IoU), and Recall, alongside model complexity. MobileNetV2 with Squeeze-and-Excitation (SCSE) achieved the best performance, with a Dice score of 0.5766 while using approximately 75% fewer parameters than U-Net. Cross-dataset evaluation on the DMID dataset showed reduced accuracy due to domain shift but preserved recall. These results demonstrate that lightweight architectures offer a practical balance between performance and efficiency for deployable CAD systems.

FaSTA$^*$: Fast-Slow Toolpath Agent with Subroutine Mining for Efficient Multi-turn Image Editing
Authors: Advait Gupta, Rishie Raj, Dang Nguyen, Tianyi Zhou
First: 2025-06-26T00:33:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:42:17+00:00
Comments: The Fourteenth International Conference on Learning Representations
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We develop a cost-efficient neurosymbolic agent to address challenging multi-turn image editing tasks such as ``Detect the bench in the image while recoloring it to pink. Also, remove the cat for a clearer view and recolor the wall to yellow.'' It combines the fast, high-level subtask planning by large language models (LLMs) with the slow, accurate, tool-use, and local A$^*$ search per subtask to find a cost-efficient toolpath -- a sequence of calls to AI tools. To save the cost of A$^*$ on similar subtasks, we perform inductive reasoning on previously successful toolpaths via LLMs to continuously extract/refine frequently used subroutines and reuse them as new tools for future tasks in an adaptive fast-slow planning, where the higher-level subroutines are explored first, and only when they fail, the low-level A$^*$ search is activated. The reusable symbolic subroutines considerably save exploration cost on the same types of subtasks applied to similar images, yielding a human-like fast-slow toolpath agent ``FaSTA$^*$'': fast subtask planning followed by rule-based subroutine selection per subtask is attempted by LLMs at first, which is expected to cover most tasks, while slow A$^*$ search is only triggered for novel and challenging subtasks. By comparing with recent image editing approaches, we demonstrate FaSTA$^*$ is significantly more computationally efficient while remaining competitive with the state-of-the-art baseline in terms of success rate. Our code and data can be accessed at https://github.com/tianyi-lab/FaSTAR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FaSTA$^*$：一种通过子程序挖掘实现高效多轮图像编辑的快慢工具路径代理
我们开发了一种成本高效的神经符号代理，用于解决具有挑战性的多轮图像编辑任务，例如“在图像中检测长凳并将其着色为粉色；同时移除猫以获得更清晰的视野，并将墙壁着色为黄色”。该代理结合了大语言模型（LLM）的快速高层子任务规划与每个子任务的慢速、精确、工具使用及局部A$^*$搜索，以寻找成本高效的工具路径——即一系列对AI工具的调用序列。为节省在相似子任务上A$^*$搜索的成本，我们通过LLM对先前成功的工具路径进行归纳推理，持续提取/优化常用子程序，并将其作为新工具在自适应的快慢规划中复用，即优先探索高层子程序，仅当其失败时才激活低层A$^*$搜索。可复用的符号子程序显著降低了在相似图像上处理同类子任务的探索成本，从而实现了类人快慢工具路径代理“FaSTA$^*$”：首先由LLM尝试快速子任务规划并基于规则选择每个子任务的子程序，预期可覆盖大部分任务；仅当遇到新颖且具有挑战性的子任务时，才触发慢速A$^*$搜索。与近期图像编辑方法相比，我们证明FaSTA$^*$在计算效率上显著提升，同时在成功率上仍与当前最先进基线保持竞争力。我们的代码和数据可在https://github.com/tianyi-lab/FaSTAR获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient multi-turn image editing, FaSTA$^*$ combines fast high-level planning via large language models with slow, accurate toolpath search using local A$^*$ to generate cost-effective sequences of AI tool calls. It introduces subroutine mining, where LLMs extract and refine reusable symbolic subroutines from prior successful toolpaths to reduce redundant exploration. When faced with a new task, FaSTA$^*$ first attempts to solve subtasks using pre-learned subroutines; only if these fail does it fall back to slow A$^*$ search. Experimental results show FaSTA$^*$ achieves competitive success rates while significantly reducing computational cost compared to recent image editing methods.
为提升多轮图像编辑的效率，作者提出FaSTA*，结合大语言模型进行快速高层子任务规划与局部A*搜索进行精确工具路径规划。通过挖掘并复用在相似任务中成功的子程序，系统实现自适应快慢规划：LLM优先尝试基于规则的子程序选择，仅在遇到新或困难子任务时才启动A*搜索。实验表明，FaSTA*在保持与最新基线相当成功率的同时，显著降低了计算开销。
Understanding In-Context Learning for Nonlinear Regression with Transformers: Attention as Featurizer
Authors: Alexander Hsu, Zhaiming Shen, Wenjing Liao, Rongjie Lai
First: 2026-05-06T17:42:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:42:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pre-trained transformers are able to learn from examples provided as part of the prompt without any weight updates, a remarkable ability known as in-context learning (ICL). Despite its demonstrated efficacy across various domains, the theoretical understanding of ICL is still developing. Whereas most existing theory has focused on linear models, we study ICL in the nonlinear regression setting. Through the interaction mechanism in attention, we explicitly construct transformer networks to realize nonlinear features, such as polynomial or spline bases, which span a wide class of functions. Based on this construction, we establish a framework to analyze end-to-end in-context nonlinear regression with the constructed features. Our theory provides finite-sample generalization error bounds in terms of context length and training set size. We numerically validate the theory on synthetic regression tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Transformer的非线性回归中上下文学习的理解：注意力作为特征提取器
预训练的Transformer能够在不更新任何权重的情况下，从提示中的示例中学习，这种非凡能力被称为上下文学习（ICL）。尽管ICL在多个领域已显示出有效性，但其理论理解仍在发展中。现有理论大多聚焦于线性模型，而我们研究的是非线性回归场景下的ICL。通过注意力机制的交互，我们显式构建Transformer网络以实现非线性特征，如多项式或样条基，这些特征覆盖了广泛的功能类。基于此构建，我们建立了一个框架，用于分析端到端的非线性回归中上下文学习。我们的理论提供了以上下文长度和训练集大小为变量的有限样本泛化误差界。我们在合成回归任务上数值验证了该理论。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand in-context learning (ICL) beyond linear models, this work investigates ICL in nonlinear regression using transformers. The authors leverage the attention mechanism to explicitly construct nonlinear feature representations—such as polynomial or spline bases—and integrate them into transformer architectures. They then derive finite-sample generalization error bounds for end-to-end ICL in this nonlinear setting, relating performance to context length and training set size. Experimental results on synthetic regression tasks confirm the theoretical predictions, demonstrating that attention-based feature construction enables effective nonlinear ICL.
为理解非线性回归场景下的上下文学习（ICL），该研究通过注意力机制显式构建包含多项式或样条基等非线性特征的Transformer网络。作者据此建立端到端非线性回归ICL的理论分析框架，推导出依赖上下文长度与训练样本量的有限样本泛化误差界。在合成回归任务上的数值实验验证了理论预测，表明注意力驱动的特征构造可在无需参数更新的情况下实现有效的ICL。
MRI-Eval: A Tiered Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Performance on MRI Physics and GE Scanner Operations Knowledge
Authors: Perry E. Radau
First: 2026-05-06T17:42:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:42:01+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 4 figures, 10 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Existing MRI LLM benchmarks rely mainly on review-book multiple-choice questions, where top proprietary models already score highly, limiting discrimination. No systematic benchmark has evaluated vendor-specific scanner operational knowledge central to research MRI practice. Purpose: We developed MRI-Eval, a tiered benchmark for relative model comparison on MRI physics and GE scanner operations knowledge using primary multiple-choice questions (MCQ), with stem-only and primed diagnostic conditions as complementary analyses. Methods: MRI-Eval includes 1365 scored items across nine categories and three difficulty tiers from textbooks, GE scanner manuals, programming course materials, and expert-generated questions. Five model families were evaluated (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Llama 3.3 70B). MCQ was primary; stem-only removed options and used an independent LLM judge; primed stem-only tested responses to incorrect user claims. Results: Overall MCQ accuracy was 93.2% to 97.1%. GE scanner operations was the lowest category for every model (88.2% to 94.6%). In stem-only, frontier-model accuracy fell to 58.4% to 61.1%, and Llama 3.3 70B fell to 37.1%; GE scanner operations stem-only accuracy was 13.8% to 29.8%. Conclusion: High MCQ performance can mask weak free-text recall, especially for vendor-specific operational knowledge. MRI-Eval is most informative as a relative comparison benchmark rather than an absolute competency measure and supports caution in using raw LLM outputs for GE-specific protocol guidance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MRI-Eval：用于评估大语言模型在MRI物理与GE扫描仪操作知识表现的分级基准
背景：现有MRI大语言模型基准主要依赖教科书式多选题，顶级专有模型已取得高分，难以区分能力差异。尚无系统性基准评估与研究MRI实践密切相关的厂商特定扫描仪操作知识。目的：我们开发了MRI-Eval，一个基于初级多选题（MCQ）的分级基准，用于相对比较模型在MRI物理与GE扫描仪操作知识上的表现，并辅以仅题干和提示性诊断条件的分析。方法：MRI-Eval包含来自教科书、GE扫描仪手册、编程课程材料及专家原创题目的1365道评分题目，涵盖九个类别和三个难度等级。评估了五类模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Llama 3.3 70B）。MCQ为主要评估方式；仅题干模式移除选项并使用独立LLM裁判；提示性题干模式测试模型对错误用户陈述的回应。结果：整体MCQ准确率在93.2%至97.1%之间。GE扫描仪操作是所有模型表现最弱的类别（88.2%至94.6%）。在仅题干模式下，前沿模型准确率降至58.4%至61.1%，Llama 3.3 70B降至37.1%；GE扫描仪操作仅题干准确率仅为13.8%至29.8%。结论：高MCQ表现可能掩盖自由文本回忆能力的薄弱，尤其在厂商特定操作知识方面。MRI-Eval作为相对比较基准更具信息价值，而非绝对能力衡量标准，支持谨慎使用LLM原始输出作为GE特定协议指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess LLMs' real-world MRI knowledge beyond superficial multiple-choice performance, MRI-Eval introduces a tiered benchmark using 1365 primary MCQs drawn from textbooks, GE manuals, and expert sources, supplemented by stem-only and primed diagnostic conditions. Five major LLM families were evaluated, showing high MCQ accuracy (93.2%–97.1%), but significant drops in stem-only mode (58.4%–61.1% for frontier models, 37.1% for Llama 3.3 70B), especially in GE scanner operations (13.8%–29.8% stem-only). This reveals that high MCQ scores mask poor free-text recall and vendor-specific operational knowledge, suggesting MRI-Eval is best suited for relative model comparison rather than absolute competency validation.
为评估LLM在MRI物理和GE扫描仪操作知识方面的实际能力，MRI-Eval构建了一个分层基准，包含1365道来自教材、手册和专家题目的多选题，按九个类别和三个难度层级划分。五类主流LLM模型在标准多选题、无选项题（由LLM评分）和带错误引导的无选项题中进行测试。结果显示，多选题准确率高达93.2%至97.1%，但自由文本回忆能力显著不足，尤其在GE操作领域（多选题88.2%–94.6%，无选项题仅13.8%–29.8%）。前沿模型如GPT-5.4和Claude Opus表现最佳，而Llama 3.3 70B在无选项题中表现极差，表明该基准更适合相对能力比较而非绝对能力评估。
Beyond Public Access in LLM Pre-Training Data
Authors: Sruly Rosenblat, Tim O'Reilly, Ilan Strauss
First: 2025-04-24T15:49:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:41:54+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 4 figures. Revised based on peer review comments. Added bootstrapped 95% CIs for all AUROC scores and z-tests comparing public vs. non-public recognition (new Table 3). Qualified claims where differences are not statistically significant at book level. Updated contact information
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Using a legally obtained dataset of 34 copyrighted O'Reilly Media books, we apply the DE-COP membership inference attack method to investigate whether OpenAI's large language models show recognition of copyrighted content. Our results based on this small sample suggest that GPT-4o, OpenAI's more recent and capable model, exhibits patterns consistent with recognition of pay-walled book content, with an AUROC score of 0.82 (95% bootstrapped CI: 0.60-0.96), though this wide confidence interval reflects substantial uncertainty due to the limited number of books tested. GPT-4o Mini, as a much smaller model, shows little recognition of any O'Reilly Media content with an AUROC score of 0.56 (0.28-0.83) for non-public data. Testing multiple models, with the same cutoff date, provides a partial control for potential language shifts over time that might bias our findings, though differences in model size, architecture, and potentially training data composition limit the strength of this control. These preliminary results underscore the importance of increased corporate transparency regarding pre-training data sources and the development of formal licensing frameworks for AI content training. Our principal contribution is our examination of public and non public data separately.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越公开访问：LLM预训练数据中的版权内容识别
我们使用合法获取的34本O'Reilly媒体版权书籍数据集，应用DE-COP成员推断攻击方法，探究OpenAI大型语言模型是否能识别受版权保护的内容。基于此小样本的结果表明，GPT-4o（OpenAI较新且能力更强的模型）表现出与识别付费墙内书籍内容一致的模式，AUROC得分为0.82（95%自举置信区间：0.60-0.96），但该较宽置信区间反映了因测试书籍数量有限而产生的较大不确定性。GPT-4o Mini作为更小规模模型，对O'Reilly媒体内容几乎无识别能力，非公开数据的AUROC得分为0.56（0.28-0.83）。在相同截止日期下测试多个模型，可部分控制因语言随时间变化可能带来的偏差，但模型规模、架构及训练数据构成的差异限制了该控制的有效性。这些初步结果强调了企业应提高预训练数据来源的透明度，并建立AI内容训练的正式授权框架的重要性。我们的主要贡献是分别考察公开与非公开数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns over copyright infringement in large language model pre-training, this study uses a legally acquired dataset of 34 O'Reilly Media books to test whether OpenAI models recognize copyrighted content via a DE-COP membership inference attack. Results indicate that GPT-4o shows statistically significant recognition of non-public content with an AUROC of 0.82 (95% CI: 0.60–0.96), while GPT-4o Mini shows minimal recognition (AUROC 0.56, 0.28–0.83). The wide confidence intervals reflect uncertainty due to the small sample size, and while model comparisons partially control for temporal language shifts, differences in architecture and training data limit their reliability. The findings highlight the need for corporate transparency and formal licensing in AI training data.
为探究大型语言模型训练数据中是否存在版权内容泄露，该研究使用合法获取的34本O'Reilly出版社书籍数据，采用DE-COP成员推断攻击方法进行测试。结果显示，GPT-4o对非公开内容表现出显著识别能力（AUROC 0.82，95%置信区间0.60–0.96），而GPT-4o Mini识别能力较弱（AUROC 0.56，0.28–0.83）。尽管样本量小导致结果不确定性高，但表明大模型更易保留训练数据痕迹。研究指出，模型架构与训练数据构成差异限制了对语言随时间变化的控制能力，呼吁提高企业数据透明度并建立AI训练数据正式授权机制。
AutoOR: Scalably Post-training LLMs to Autoformalize Operations Research Problems
Authors: Sumeet Ramesh Motwani, Chuan Du, Aleksander Petrov, Christopher Davis, Philip Torr, Antonio Papania-Davis, Weishi Yan
First: 2026-04-18T03:24:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:41:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optimization problems are central to decision-making in manufacturing, logistics, scheduling, and other industrial settings. Translating complicated descriptions of these problems into solver-ready formulations requires specialized operations research (OR) expertise, making it hard to scale. We present AutoOR, a scalable synthetic data generation and reinforcement learning pipeline that trains LLMs to autoformalize optimization problems specified in natural language across linear, mixed-integer, and non-linear categories. AutoOR generates verified training data from standard optimization forms and uses solver execution feedback as the reward signal for RL post-training. AutoOR applied to an 8B model achieves state-of-the-art or competitive results across six established OR benchmarks, matching significantly larger frontier models. For a non-linear problem class involving physical dynamics, where frontier models score near 0%, we introduce a curriculum RL strategy that bootstraps from limited initial training data to make this class tractable for post-training. We believe that methods such as AutoOR can significantly accelerate industrial decision-making with AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AutoOR：可扩展的后训练大语言模型自动形式化运筹学问题
优化问题在制造、物流、调度等工业场景的决策中至关重要。将这些问题的复杂自然语言描述转化为求解器可处理的形式，需要专门的运筹学（OR）专业知识，难以规模化。我们提出AutoOR，一种可扩展的合成数据生成与强化学习管道，训练大语言模型自动形式化以自然语言指定的优化问题，涵盖线性、混合整数和非线性类别。AutoOR从标准优化形式生成验证过的训练数据，并利用求解器执行反馈作为后训练强化学习的奖励信号。AutoOR应用于8B模型，在六个经典OR基准测试中达到最先进或竞争性结果，性能显著优于更大规模的前沿模型。针对涉及物理动态的非线性问题类别（前沿模型得分接近0%），我们引入课程式强化学习策略，从有限初始训练数据中自举，使该类别在后训练中变得可行。我们认为，此类方法如AutoOR可显著加速工业决策中的AI应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of scaling operations research problem formalization due to specialized expertise requirements, AutoOR employs a synthetic data generation and reinforcement learning pipeline to train large language models to autoformalize optimization problems from natural language. The method generates verified training data from standard optimization forms and uses solver execution feedback as a reward signal for post-training reinforcement learning. Experimental results show that AutoOR achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on six OR benchmarks using an 8B model, outperforming or matching larger frontier models, and successfully handles previously intractable non-linear problems via a curriculum RL strategy that bootstraps from limited initial data.
为解决因专业能力限制导致的运筹学问题形式化难以规模化的问题，AutoOR采用合成数据生成与强化学习管道，训练大语言模型从自然语言自动形式化优化问题。该方法从标准优化形式生成验证数据，并以求解器执行反馈作为强化学习的奖励信号。实验表明，AutoOR在六个运筹学基准测试中达到最先进或竞争性水平，仅用8B模型即可媲美更大规模的前沿模型，并通过课程式强化学习策略成功处理此前几乎无法解决的非线性问题类别，使其性能从接近零提升至可实现水平。
SegMix:Shuffle-based Feedback Learning for Semantic Segmentation of Pathology Images
Authors: Zhiling Yan, Sicheng Chen, Tianyi Zhang, Nan Ying, Yanli Lei, Guanglei Zhang
First: 2026-04-17T07:33:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:40:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Segmentation is a critical task in computational pathology, as it identifies areas affected by disease or abnormal growth and is essential for diagnosis and treatment. However, acquiring high-quality pixel-level supervised segmentation data requires significant workload demands from experienced pathologists, limiting the application of deep learning. To overcome this challenge, relaxing the label conditions to image-level classification labels allows for more data to be used and more scenarios to be enabled. One approach is to leverage Class Activation Map (CAM) to generate pseudo pixel-level annotations for semantic segmentation with only image-level labels. However, this method fails to thoroughly explore the essential characteristics of pathology images, thus identifying only small areas that are insufficient for pseudo masking. In this paper, we propose a novel shuffle-based feedback learning method inspired by curriculum learning to generate higher-quality pseudo-semantic segmentation masks. Specifically, we perform patch level shuffle of pathology images, with the model adaptively adjusting the shuffle strategy based on feedback from previous learning. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-arts on three different datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SegMix：基于洗牌反馈学习的病理图像语义分割方法
分割是计算病理学中的关键任务，用于识别受疾病或异常生长影响的区域，对诊断和治疗至关重要。然而，获取高质量像素级监督分割数据需要经验丰富的病理学家投入大量工作，限制了深度学习的应用。为解决此问题，放宽标签条件至图像级分类标签，可使用更多数据并支持更多场景。一种方法是利用类激活图（CAM）仅通过图像级标签生成伪像素级标注用于语义分割。但该方法未能充分挖掘病理图像的本质特征，仅能识别小区域，不足以用于伪掩码。本文提出一种受课程学习启发的基于洗牌的反馈学习方法，以生成更高质量的伪语义分割掩码。具体而言，我们对病理图像进行块级洗牌，模型根据先前学习的反馈自适应调整洗牌策略。实验结果表明，所提方法在三个不同数据集上均优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of pixel-level annotations in pathology image segmentation, this paper proposes SegMix, a shuffle-based feedback learning method that leverages image-level labels to generate high-quality pseudo masks. The method shuffles image patches and dynamically adjusts the shuffle strategy based on model feedback, mimicking curriculum learning to progressively refine mask quality. Experiments across three datasets show that SegMix achieves superior segmentation performance compared to existing state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in reducing annotation dependency while maintaining accuracy.
为了解决病理图像语义分割中像素级标注稀缺的问题，本文提出SegMix方法，通过基于图像级标签生成高质量伪掩码。该方法对图像块进行洗牌，并根据模型反馈自适应调整洗牌策略，模拟课程学习以逐步提升掩码质量。在三个数据集上的实验表明，SegMix在分割性能上优于现有最先进方法，有效降低了标注依赖性的同时保持了高精度。
Distributional Principal Autoencoders
Authors: Xinwei Shen, Nicolai Meinshausen
First: 2024-04-21T12:52:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:40:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dimension reduction techniques usually lose information in the sense that reconstructed data are not identical to the original data. However, we argue that it is possible to have reconstructed data identically distributed as the original data, irrespective of the retained dimension or the specific mapping. This can be achieved by learning a distributional model that matches the conditional distribution of data given its low-dimensional latent variables. Motivated by this, we propose Distributional Principal Autoencoder (DPA) that consists of an encoder that maps high-dimensional data to low-dimensional latent variables and a decoder that maps the latent variables back to the data space. For reducing the dimension, the DPA encoder aims to minimise the unexplained variability of the data with an adaptive choice of the latent dimension. For reconstructing data, the DPA decoder aims to match the conditional distribution of all data that are mapped to a certain latent value, thus ensuring that the reconstructed data retains the original data distribution. Our numerical results on climate data, single-cell data, and image benchmarks demonstrate the practical feasibility and success of the approach in reconstructing the original distribution of the data. DPA embeddings are shown to preserve meaningful structures of data such as the seasonal cycle for precipitations and cell types for gene expression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分布主自编码器
降维技术通常会丢失信息，表现为重建数据与原始数据不完全一致。然而，我们认为，无论保留的维度或具体映射如何，都可能使重建数据与原始数据具有相同的分布。这可通过学习一个匹配数据给定低维潜在变量条件分布的分布模型实现。受此启发，我们提出分布主自编码器（DPA），它包含一个将高维数据映射到低维潜在变量的编码器，以及一个将潜在变量映射回数据空间的解码器。为降维，DPA编码器旨在通过自适应选择潜在维度最小化数据的未解释变异性；为重建数据，DPA解码器旨在匹配映射到特定潜在值的所有数据的条件分布，从而确保重建数据保留原始数据分布。我们在气候数据、单细胞数据和图像基准上的数值结果表明，该方法在重建数据原始分布方面具有实用性和成功性。DPA嵌入被证明能保留数据有意义的结构，如降水的季节循环和基因表达的细胞类型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that traditional dimension reduction methods lose information by producing non-identical reconstructions, the authors propose Distributional Principal Autoencoder (DPA) to reconstruct data whose distribution matches the original, regardless of latent dimension. DPA uses an encoder to map high-dimensional data to low-dimensional latent variables while minimizing unexplained variability, and a decoder that learns to reproduce the conditional distribution of data given each latent value, ensuring distributional fidelity. Experiments on climate, single-cell, and image datasets show DPA successfully preserves original data distributions and meaningful structures such as seasonal precipitation cycles and cell-type clusters.
为解决传统降维方法因重建数据与原始数据不一致而丢失信息的问题，本文提出分布型主自编码器（DPA），通过学习数据给定低维潜在变量的条件分布，使重建数据与原始数据分布一致。DPA编码器通过最小化未解释方差选择潜在维度，解码器则匹配每个潜在值对应的条件分布以保证重建分布一致性。在气候、单细胞和图像数据上的实验表明，DPA能有效重建原始分布并保留季节性降水循环和细胞类型等有意义结构。
When Life Gives You BC, Make Q-functions: Extracting Q-values from Behavior Cloning for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Lakshita Dodeja, Ondrej Biza, Shivam Vats, Stephen Hart, Stefanie Tellex, Robin Walters, Karl Schmeckpeper, Thomas Weng
First: 2026-05-06T17:40:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:40:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Behavior Cloning (BC) has emerged as a highly effective paradigm for robot learning. However, BC lacks a self-guided mechanism for online improvement after demonstrations have been collected. Existing offline-to-online learning methods often cause policies to replace previously learned good actions due to a distribution mismatch between offline data and online learning. In this work, we propose Q2RL, Q-Estimation and Q-Gating from BC for Reinforcement Learning, an algorithm for efficient offline-to-online learning. Our method consists of two parts: (1) Q-Estimation extracts a Q-function from a BC policy using a few interaction steps with the environment, followed by online RL with (2) Q-Gating, which switches between BC and RL policy actions based on their respective Q-values to collect samples for RL policy training. Across manipulation tasks from D4RL and robomimic benchmarks, Q2RL outperforms SOTA offline-to-online learning baselines on success rate and time to convergence. Q2RL is efficient enough to be applied in an on-robot RL setting, learning robust policies for contact-rich and high precision manipulation tasks such as pipe assembly and kitting, in 1-2 hours of online interaction, achieving success rates of up to 100% and up to 3.75x improvement against the original BC policy. Code and video are available at https://pages.rai-inst.com/q2rl_website/
中文标题/摘要
标题：当生活给你BC时，就做Q函数：从行为克隆中提取Q值用于机器人上的强化学习
行为克隆（BC）已成为机器人学习中一种高效范式。然而，BC缺乏在收集演示后进行在线自我改进的机制。现有离线到在线学习方法常因离线数据与在线学习分布不匹配，导致策略替换先前学到的良好动作。本文提出Q2RL（从BC中提取Q值用于强化学习），一种高效的离线到在线学习算法。该方法包含两部分：(1) Q估计：通过少量环境交互从BC策略中提取Q函数，随后结合(2) Q门控：根据BC与RL策略的Q值切换动作，以收集RL策略训练样本。在D4RL和robomimic基准的操控任务中，Q2RL在成功率和收敛时间上优于现有离线到在线学习基线。Q2RL效率足以应用于机器人上的强化学习，仅需1-2小时在线交互，即可学习接触密集且高精度操控任务（如管件装配和套件组装）的鲁棒策略，成功率高达100%，相较原始BC策略提升高达3.75倍。代码与视频见https://pages.rai-inst.com/q2rl_website/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve upon Behavior Cloning’s lack of self-guided online learning, the authors propose Q2RL, a method that extracts Q-values from a BC policy and uses them to guide online reinforcement learning. The approach consists of Q-Estimation, which learns a Q-function from limited online interactions, and Q-Gating, which dynamically selects actions based on Q-values from either BC or RL policies to enhance sample efficiency. Experiments on manipulation tasks from D4RL and robomimic show Q2RL achieves higher success rates and faster convergence than existing offline-to-online methods, with on-robot results demonstrating 100% success in tasks like pipe assembly and kitting within 1-2 hours of online interaction, yielding up to 3.75x performance gains over original BC policies.
为解决行为克隆缺乏在线自适应优化的问题，本文提出Q2RL方法，通过少量在线交互从BC策略中提取Q函数，并结合Q-Gating机制根据Q值动态选择BC或RL动作以采样训练RL策略。在D4RL和robomimic基准任务中，Q2RL在成功率和收敛速度上优于现有最先进方法，在接触密集型精细操作任务（如管件装配和套件组装）中，仅需1–2小时在线交互即可实现100%成功率，性能较原始BC策略提升最高达3.75倍。
Design Conductor 2.0: An agent builds a TurboQuant inference accelerator in 80 hours
Authors: The Verkor Team, Ravi Krishna, Suresh Krishna, David Chin
First: 2026-05-06T17:40:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:40:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Driven by a rapid co-evolution of both harness and underlying models, LLM agents are improving at a dizzying pace. In our prior work (performed in Dec. 2025), we introduced "Design Conductor" (or just "Conductor"), a system capable of building a 5-stage Linux-capable RISC-V CPU in 12 hours. In this work, we introduce an updated multi-agent harness powered by frontier models released in April 2026, which is able to handle 80x larger tasks, at higher quality, fully autonomously. Following a brief introduction, we examine 4 designs that the system produced autonomously, including "VerTQ", an LLM inference accelerator which hard-wires support for TurboQuant in a 240-cycle pipeline, starting from the TurboQuant arXiv paper. VerTQ includes heavy compute processing, with 5129 FP16/32 units; the design was mapped to an FPGA at 125 MHz and consumes 5.7 mm^2 in TSMC 16FF (8 attention pipes). We review the key new characteristics that enabled these results. Finally, we analyze Design Conductor's token usage and other empirical characteristics, including its limitations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：设计指挥官2.0：一个代理在80小时内构建了TurboQuant推理加速器

Motivated by the rapid co-evolution of LLM agents and underlying models, this work updates the Design Conductor system to autonomously build complex hardware designs in 80 hours, up from 12 hours previously.
