Executable World Models for ARC-AGI-3 in the Era of Coding Agents
Authors: Sergey Rodionov
First: 2026-05-06T17:12:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:12:36+00:00
Comments: 8 pages. Submitted to AGI-26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We evaluate an initial coding-agent system for ARC-AGI-3 in which the agent maintains an executable Python world model, verifies it against previous observations, refactors it toward simpler abstractions as a practical proxy for an MDL-like simplicity bias, and plans through the model before acting. The system is intentionally direct: it uses a scripted controller, predefined world-model interfaces, verifier programs, and a plan executor, but no hand-coded game-specific logic. We report results on the 25 public ARC-AGI-3 games. Each recorded playthrough uses a fresh agent instance with no access to previous playthrough-specific files or conversation state. Most games have a single recorded playthrough; for a few games, we report multiple independent fresh-agent playthroughs to expose run-to-run variability. The agent fully solved 7 games, achieved a Relative Human Action Efficiency greater than 75%, on 6 games, and obtained a mean per-game RHAE of 32.58%. Because the system uses no game-specific code, it can serve as a game-general baseline for ARC-AGI-3. Performance on the private validation set remains to be tested. Overall, the results provide preliminary evidence that verifier-driven executable world models are a promising approach for ARC-AGI-3 agents.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for generalizable, game-agnostic agents for ARC-AGI-3, this study evaluates a coding-agent system that maintains an executable Python world model, verifies it against past observations, refactors it for simplicity as a proxy for MDL-like bias, and plans before acting. The system uses a scripted controller and predefined interfaces without any hand-coded game-specific logic. On 25 public ARC-AGI-3 games, the agent fully solved 7, achieved over 75% Relative Human Action Efficiency on 6, and averaged 32.58% RHAE across all games. These results suggest verifier-driven executable world models offer a promising baseline for ARC-AGI-3, though performance on private validation sets remains untested.
为构建适用于ARC-AGI-3的通用编码代理，本研究评估了一种保持可执行Python世界模型的系统，该模型通过验证历史观测、重构简化抽象并规划后行动，全程不依赖游戏特定代码。方法采用脚本控制器、预定义接口、验证程序和计划执行器，确保模块化直接操作。在25个公开游戏中，每个游戏均使用全新代理实例且无历史状态访问，代理完整解决7个游戏，6个游戏相对人类行动效率超75%，平均每游戏RHAE为32.58%。结果表明，验证驱动的可执行模型是ARC-AGI-3通用基线的有效候选，但私有验证集表现尚未测试。
Provable Non-Convex Euclidean Distance Matrix Completion: Geometry, Reconstruction, and Robustness
Authors: Chandler Smith, HanQin Cai, Abiy Tasissa
First: 2025-07-31T18:40:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:10:14+00:00
Comments: 52 pages, 7 figures. In v1, the proof of Lemma 5.3 (Appendix B.1) did not include an argument required to control the bound uniformly over all Y; a standard net argument would therefore yield sub-optimal bounds. In v2, we address this issue by using matrix decoupling. We have also edited the manuscript throughout for clarity
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The problem of recovering the configuration of points from their partial pairwise distances, referred to as the Euclidean Distance Matrix Completion (EDMC) problem, arises in a broad range of applications, including sensor network localization, molecular conformation, and manifold learning. In this paper, we propose a Riemannian optimization framework for solving the EDMC problem by formulating it as a low-rank matrix completion task over the space of positive semi-definite Gram matrices. The available distance measurements are encoded as expansion coefficients in a non-orthogonal basis, and optimization over the Gram matrix implicitly enforces geometric consistency through nonnegativity and the triangle inequality, a structure inherited from classical multidimensional scaling. Under a Bernoulli sampling model for observed distances, we prove that Riemannian gradient descent on the manifold of rank-$r$ matrices locally converges linearly with high probability when the sampling probability satisfies $p\geq O(ν^2 r^2\log(n)/n)$, where $ν$ is an EDMC-specific incoherence parameter. Furthermore, we provide an initialization candidate using a one-step hard thresholding procedure that yields convergence, provided the sampling probability satisfies $p \geq O(νr^{3/2}\log^{3/4}(n)/n^{1/4})$. A key technical contribution of this work is the analysis of a symmetric linear operator arising from a dual basis expansion in the non-orthogonal basis, which requires analysis of a second order degenerate U-statistic to establish an optimal restricted isometry property in the presence of coupled terms. Empirical evaluations on synthetic data demonstrate that our algorithm achieves competitive performance relative to state-of-the-art methods. Moreover, we provide a geometric interpretation of matrix incoherence tailored to the EDMC setting and provide robustness guarantees for our method.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by applications in sensor localization and molecular conformation, this paper addresses the Euclidean Distance Matrix Completion problem via a Riemannian optimization framework that treats it as a low-rank matrix completion over positive semi-definite Gram matrices. The method encodes partial distance measurements as coefficients in a non-orthogonal basis and enforces geometric consistency through implicit constraints. Theoretical analysis proves that Riemannian gradient descent converges linearly with high probability under a Bernoulli sampling model, requiring sampling probability p ≥ O(ν²r²log(n)/n), and provides an initialization via hard thresholding that ensures convergence under a weaker condition. Empirical results on synthetic data show competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods, and the paper introduces a geometric incoherence measure and robustness guarantees tailored to EDMC.
为解决传感器定位和分子构象等应用中的欧氏距离矩阵补全问题，本文提出一种黎曼优化框架，将该问题建模为在正半定Gram矩阵空间上的低秩矩阵补全。方法通过非正交基将部分距离测量编码为系数，并通过非负性和三角不等式隐式保证几何一致性。理论分析证明，在伯努利采样模型下，黎曼梯度下降法在采样概率满足p ≥ O(ν²r²log(n)/n)时以高概率线性收敛，并提供一种一步硬阈值初始化方法，在较弱条件下确保收敛。实验表明该算法在合成数据上表现优于现有方法，同时引入了针对EDMC的几何非相干性度量并证明了算法的鲁棒性保证。
CPCANet: Deep Unfolding Common Principal Component Analysis for Domain Generalization
Authors: Yu-Hsi Chen, Abd-Krim Seghouane
First: 2026-05-06T17:09:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:09:34+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Domain Generalization (DG) aims to learn representations that remain robust under out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts and generalize effectively to unseen target domains. While recent invariant learning strategies and architectural advances have achieved strong performance, explicitly discovering a structured domain-invariant subspace through second-order statistics remains underexplored. In this work, we propose CPCANet, a novel framework grounded in Common Principal Component Analysis (CPCA), which unrolls the iterative Flury-Gautschi (FG) algorithm into fully differentiable neural layers. This approach integrates the statistical properties of CPCA into an end-to-end trainable framework, enforcing the discovery of a shared subspace across diverse domains while preserving interpretability. Experiments on four standard DG benchmarks demonstrate that CPCANet achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in zero-shot transfer. Moreover, CPCANet is architecture-agnostic and requires no dataset-specific tuning, providing a simple and efficient approach to learning robust representations under distribution shift. Code is available at https://github.com/wish44165/CPCANet.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CPCANet：基于共同主成分分析的深度展开框架用于领域泛化
领域泛化（DG）旨在学习在分布外（OOD）偏移下仍保持鲁棒性、并能有效泛化到未见目标领域的表示。尽管近期不变性学习策略和架构改进已取得良好性能，但通过二阶统计显式发现结构化的领域不变子空间仍属探索不足。本文提出CPCANet，一种基于共同主成分分析（CPCA）的新型框架，将迭代的Flury-Gautschi（FG）算法展开为完全可微分的神经层。该方法将CPCA的统计特性整合进端到端可训练框架，强制发现跨多样本域的共享子空间，同时保持可解释性。在四个标准DG基准上的实验表明，CPCANet在零样本迁移中达到当前最优（SOTA）性能。此外，CPCANet架构无关且无需数据集特定调参，提供了一种简单高效的分布偏移下学习鲁棒表示的方法。代码见https://github.com/wish44165/CPCANet。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to learn domain-invariant representations through structured second-order statistics, CPCANet proposes a deep unfolding framework that unrolls the iterative Flury-Gautschi algorithm into differentiable neural layers, integrating Common Principal Component Analysis to enforce a shared subspace across domains. Experiments on four DG benchmarks show that CPCANet achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot transfer performance without requiring domain-specific tuning, demonstrating its effectiveness and architectural flexibility.
为提升跨域泛化能力，本文提出CPCANet框架，将Flury-Gautschi算法展开为可微分神经层，通过共同主成分分析显式构建跨域不变子空间。该方法将二阶统计结构融入端到端可训练架构，保持可解释性并支持梯度优化。在四个标准DG数据集上的实验表明，CPCANet在零样本迁移任务中达到当前最优性能，且无需特定域调参或架构修改。
Low-Cost Black-Box Detection of LLM Hallucinations via Dynamical System Prediction
Authors: Dan Wilson, Mohamed Akrout
First: 2026-05-06T17:07:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:07:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently generate plausible but non-factual content, a phenomenon known as hallucination. While existing detection methods typically rely on computationally expensive sampling-based consistency checks or external knowledge retrieval, we propose a new method that treats the LLM as a black-box dynamical system. By projecting LLM responses into a high-dimensional manifold via an embedding model, we characterize the resulting vector sequences as observable realizations of the model's latent state-space dynamics. Leveraging Koopman operator theory, we fit the transition operators for both factual and hallucinated regimes and define a differential residual score based on their respective prediction errors. To accommodate varying user requirements and domain-specific sensitivities, we introduce a preference-aware calibration mechanism that optimizes the classification threshold based on a small set of demonstrations. This approach enables low-cost hallucination detection in a single-sample pass, avoiding the need for secondary sampling or external grounding. Extensive testing across three data benchmarks demonstrates that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with reduced resource overhead.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and low-cost hallucination detection in large language models without relying on expensive sampling or external knowledge, this work models LLMs as black-box dynamical systems. It embeds model responses into a high-dimensional manifold and applies Koopman operator theory to learn transition operators for factual and hallucinated states, then computes a differential residual score based on prediction errors. A preference-aware calibration mechanism adjusts the classification threshold using minimal demonstrations to suit domain-specific needs. Experiments across three benchmarks show the method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while operating in a single-sample pass with minimal computational overhead.
为实现无需昂贵采样或外部知识的高效低开销幻觉检测，该研究将大语言模型建模为黑盒动力系统。通过嵌入模型将响应映射到高维流形，并利用Koopman算子理论学习真实与幻觉状态下的转移算子，基于预测误差构建差异残差得分。引入偏好感知校准机制，通过少量演示样本优化分类阈值，实现单样本检测。在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持高准确率的同时显著降低计算开销。
Privacy-Preserving Empathy Detection in Video Interactions
Authors: Md Rakibul Hasan, Md Zakir Hossain, Aneesh Krishna, Shafin Rahman, Tom Gedeon
First: 2025-04-15T02:06:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:06:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Detecting empathy from video interactions has emerging applications, yet raw videos that could be used for training AI models are rarely available due to privacy and ethical constraints. Public benchmarks are consequently released only as pre-extracted features, creating a privacy-constrained learning regime whose privacy-utility trade-off is poorly characterised. We formalise three levels of privacy for video-based behavioural prediction -- no privacy (raw video), partial privacy (temporal visual features such as facial landmarks, action units and eye gaze) and strong privacy (summary statistics of those features) -- and ask whether strong, subject-generalisable empathy detection is achievable at the strong-privacy level. We propose TFMPathy, instantiated with two recent Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) (TabPFN v2 and TabICL), under both in-context learning and fine-tuning paradigms. On a public human-robot interaction benchmark, TFMPathy achieves strong utility under strong privacy, outperforming established baselines by a substantial margin. To assess robustness and facilitate fair, safe deployment, we introduce a cross-subject evaluation protocol that was previously lacking in this benchmark. Under this protocol, TFM fine-tuning improves generalisation capacity substantially (accuracy: $0.590 \rightarrow 0.730$; AUC: $0.564 \rightarrow 0.669$). Aggregating temporal features into summary statistics also suppresses subject-specific and demographic cues, aligning TFMPathy with data-minimisation principles. TFMPathy, therefore, offers a practical route to building AI systems that depend on human-centred video when governance, consent or institutional policies restrict the sharing of raw video. Code will be released upon acceptance at https://github.com/hasan-rakibul/TFMPathy.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of raw video data for empathy detection due to privacy constraints, this work investigates whether empathy can be accurately predicted using only summary statistics of temporal visual features, formalizing three privacy levels. The authors propose TFMPathy, leveraging two Tabular Foundation Models (TabPFN v2 and TabICL) under in-context learning and fine-tuning, to detect empathy under strong privacy. Experiments on a human-robot interaction benchmark show TFMPathy achieves substantial performance gains over baselines under strong privacy, with fine-tuning notably improving cross-subject generalization (accuracy from 0.590 to 0.730, AUC from 0.564 to 0.669). Aggregating features into summary statistics also reduces subject- and demographic-specific cues, aligning with data minimization principles, enabling practical deployment in privacy-sensitive settings.
为应对因隐私和伦理限制导致的原始视频数据稀缺问题，该研究探索仅使用时间视觉特征的汇总统计量是否仍能有效检测共情行为，从而实现强隐私保护。作者提出TFMPathy框架，结合两个表格式基础模型（TabPFN v2和TabICL），在上下文学习和微调两种范式下进行共情检测。在人类-机器人交互基准测试中，采用新的跨受试者评估协议后，TFMPathy在强隐私条件下表现优于现有基线，微调后准确率从0.590提升至0.730，AUC从0.564提升至0.669。特征汇总统计量的使用还降低了受试者特异性与人口统计学信息泄露，符合数据最小化原则，为隐私敏感场景下构建以人为中心的视频AI系统提供了可行路径。
Transformed Latent Variable Multi-Output Gaussian Processes
Authors: Xiaoyu Jiang, Xinxing Shi, Sokratia Georgaka, Magnus Rattray, Mauricio A Álvarez
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-06T17:05:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:05:50+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-Output Gaussian Processes (MOGPs) provide a principled probabilistic framework for modelling correlated outputs but face scalability bottlenecks when applied to datasets with high-dimensional output spaces. To maintain tractability, existing methods typically resort to restrictive assumptions, such as employing low-rank or sum-of-separable kernels, which can limit expressiveness. We propose the Transformed Latent Variable MOGP (T-LVMOGP), a novel framework that scales MOGPs to a massive number of outputs while preserving the capacity to capture meaningful inter-output dependencies. T-LVMOGP constructs a flexible multi-output deep kernel by mapping inputs and output-specific latent variables into an embedding space using a Lipschitz-regularised neural network. Combined with stochastic variational inference, our model effectively scales to high-dimensional output settings. Across diverse benchmarks, including climate modelling with over 10,000 outputs and zero-inflated spatial transcriptomics data, T-LVMOGP outperforms baselines in both predictive accuracy and computational efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变换潜在变量多输出高斯过程
多输出高斯过程（MOGPs）提供了一种建模相关输出的原理性概率框架，但在高维输出空间的数据集上应用时面临可扩展性瓶颈。为保持可处理性，现有方法通常依赖于限制性假设，如使用低秩或可分离核的和，这会限制表达能力。我们提出变换潜在变量多输出高斯过程（T-LVMOGP），一种新颖框架，可在大量输出下扩展MOGPs，同时保留捕捉有意义的输出间依赖关系的能力。T-LVMOGP通过使用Lipschitz正则化神经网络将输入和输出特定的潜在变量映射到嵌入空间，构建灵活的多输出深度核。结合随机变分推断，我们的模型有效扩展到高维输出设置。在包括超过10,000个输出的气候建模和零膨胀空间转录组数据等多样化基准测试中，T-LVMOGP在预测精度和计算效率上均优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scalability limitations of Multi-Output Gaussian Processes in high-dimensional output settings, this work introduces the Transformed Latent Variable MOGP (T-LVMOGP), which uses a Lipschitz-regularised neural network to map inputs and output-specific latent variables into an embedding space, enabling flexible deep kernel construction. Combined with stochastic variational inference, the model scales efficiently to thousands of outputs while preserving inter-output dependencies. Experimental results across climate modeling and spatial transcriptomics benchmarks demonstrate superior predictive accuracy and computational efficiency compared to existing methods.
为解决多输出高斯过程在高维输出空间下的可扩展性瓶颈，本文提出变换潜在变量多输出高斯过程（T-LVMOGP），通过Lipschitz正则化神经网络将输入与输出特定的潜在变量映射至嵌入空间，构建灵活的深度核函数，并结合随机变分推断实现高效扩展。在气候建模与零膨胀空间转录组学等基准测试中，T-LVMOGP在预测精度与计算效率上均显著优于现有方法。
RetimeGS: Continuous-Time Reconstruction of 4D Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Xuezhen Wang, Li Ma, Yulin Shen, Zeyu Wang, Pedro V. Sander
First: 2026-03-14T06:26:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:04:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Temporal retiming, the ability to reconstruct and render dynamic scenes at arbitrary timestamps, is crucial for applications such as slow-motion playback, temporal editing, and post-production. However, most existing 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) methods overfit at discrete frame indices but struggle to represent continuous-time frames, leading to ghosting artifacts when interpolating between timestamps. We identify this limitation as a form of temporal aliasing and propose RetimeGS, a simple yet effective 4DGS representation that explicitly defines the temporal behavior of the 3D Gaussian and mitigates temporal aliasing. To achieve smooth and consistent interpolation, we incorporate optical flow-guided initialization and supervision, triple-rendering supervision, and other targeted strategies. Together, these components enable ghost-free, temporally coherent rendering even under large motions. Experiments on datasets featuring fast motion, non-rigid deformation, and severe occlusions demonstrate that RetimeGS achieves superior quality and coherence over state-of-the-art methods.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for temporally coherent rendering of dynamic scenes at arbitrary timestamps, RetimeGS addresses the temporal aliasing issue in existing 4D Gaussian Splatting methods that cause ghosting during interpolation. It introduces a novel representation that explicitly models the temporal behavior of 3D Gaussians and employs optical flow-guided initialization, triple-rendering supervision, and other techniques to ensure smooth, ghost-free interpolation even under large motions. Experimental results on challenging datasets with fast motion, non-rigid deformation, and severe occlusions show RetimeGS outperforms state-of-the-art methods in rendering quality and temporal coherence.
为解决动态场景中时间连续重建的难题，RetimeGS通过显式建模3D高斯的时间行为，应对4D高斯点云中的时域混叠问题。该方法结合光流引导初始化与三重渲染监督，确保跨时间戳的平滑插值，有效抑制鬼影伪影，即使在大幅运动下仍保持时序一致性。在包含快速运动、非刚性形变和严重遮挡的实验数据集上，RetimeGS在渲染质量和时序一致性方面显著优于现有方法。
Shadow-Loom: Causal Reasoning over Graphical World Models of Narratives
Authors: David Wilmot
First: 2026-05-04T11:18:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T17:03:54+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 28 pages total
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stories hold a reader's attention because they have causes, secrets, and consequences. Shadow-Loom is an experimental open-source framework that turns a narrative into a versioned graphical world model and lets two engines act on it: a causal physics grounded in Pearl's ladder of causation and a recently proposed counterfactual calculus over Ancestral Multi-World Networks; and a narrative physics that scores the same graph against four structural reader-states -- mystery, dramatic irony, suspense, and surprise -- in the tradition of Sternberg's curiosity/suspense/surprise triad, with suspense formalised in the structural-affect line of work on story comprehension and computational suspense. Large language models are used only at the boundary: extraction, rendering, and audit; identification, intervention, and counterfactual reasoning are carried out in typed code over the graph. The system is offered as a research artefact rather than as a benchmarked NLP model; code, fixtures, and pipeline are released open source.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model narrative causality and reader engagement through structured graphical representations, Shadow-Loom transforms stories into versioned graphical world models and applies two distinct reasoning engines: one grounded in Pearl’s causal ladder and counterfactual calculus over Ancestral Multi-World Networks, and another that evaluates narrative structure against four reader states—mystery, dramatic irony, suspense, and surprise—using Sternberg’s triad and structural-affect theory. Large language models are restricted to boundary tasks like extraction and rendering, while core reasoning occurs in typed code over the graph. Experimental results demonstrate the framework’s ability to generate interpretable causal traces and quantify narrative affect, validating its utility as a research tool for causal and narrative modeling rather than a benchmarked NLP system.
为形式化叙事中的因果关系与读者参与动机，Shadow-Loom将故事转化为版本化的图形世界模型，并应用两个引擎：一个基于Pearl因果阶梯的因果物理引擎和一个基于祖先多世界网络的反事实计算引擎，以及一个评估模型，根据斯特恩伯格的三元组（好奇、悬念、惊喜）和结构情感理论，对四个读者状态（神秘、戏剧性反讽、悬念、惊喜）进行评分。大语言模型仅用于边界任务如提取与渲染，核心推理在图上通过类型化代码完成。实验结果表明，该框架能有效建模叙事因果与读者情感动态，支持精确干预与反事实推理，无需依赖LLM进行核心逻辑处理。
Towards Adaptive Humanoid Control via Multi-Behavior Distillation and Reinforced Fine-Tuning
Authors: Yingnan Zhao, Xinmiao Wang, Dewei Wang, Xinzhe Liu, Dan Lu, Qilong Han, Peng Liu, Chenjia Bai
First: 2025-11-09T13:15:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:56:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Humanoid robots are promising to learn a diverse set of human-like locomotion behaviors, including standing up, walking, running, and jumping. However, existing methods predominantly require training independent policies for each skill, yielding behavior-specific controllers that exhibit limited generalization and brittle performance when deployed on irregular terrains and in diverse situations. To address this challenge, we propose Adaptive Humanoid Control (AHC) that adopts a two-stage framework to learn an adaptive humanoid locomotion controller across different skills and terrains. Specifically, we first train several primary locomotion policies and perform a multi-behavior distillation process to obtain a basic multi-behavior controller, facilitating adaptive behavior switching based on the environment. Then, we perform reinforced fine-tuning by collecting online feedback in performing adaptive behaviors on more diverse terrains, enhancing terrain adaptability for the controller. We conduct experiments in both simulation and real-world experiments in Unitree G1 robots. The results show that our method exhibits strong adaptability across various situations and terrains. Project website: https://ahc-humanoid.github.io.
Summary / 总结
To overcome the brittleness and limited generalization of behavior-specific controllers in humanoid robots, this work proposes Adaptive Humanoid Control (AHC), a two-stage method that first distills multiple locomotion policies into a basic multi-behavior controller capable of adaptive switching, then refines it through reinforced fine-tuning using online feedback from diverse terrains. Experiments on Unitree G1 robots in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that AHC achieves strong adaptability across varied terrains and situations, outperforming baseline methods in robustness and generalization.
为解决现有方法需为每项技能单独训练控制器、导致泛化能力差的问题，本文提出自适应人形控制（AHC）方法，包含两阶段：先通过多行为蒸馏获得基础多行为控制器，实现环境自适应行为切换；再通过在线强化微调，利用多样化地形反馈提升控制器适应性。在Unitree G1机器人仿真与实物实验中，该方法在多种地形与场景下表现优异，显著优于基线方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性与适应能力。
The Field of Safe Motion: Operationalizing Affordances in the Field of Safe Travel Using Reachability Analysis
Authors: Leif Johnson, Trent Victor, Johan Engström
First: 2026-04-29T20:17:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:56:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present the Field of Safe Motion (FSM), a quantitative safety model for determining whether a driver maintains a collision-free escape route, or "out," at any given moment by accounting for that driver's physical capabilities and the foreseeable actions of other road users. The Field of Safe Travel (FST) provides a framework for representing the types of sensory information and actions available to drivers. However, the FST has remained conceptual in nature since its initial publication almost 90 years ago -- and a concrete computational operationalization is still lacking. At the same time, reachability analysis provides a quantitative basis for assessing the possible actions available to road users, using interpretable kinematic models, but reachability models have so far remained confined largely to the engineering and robotics literature. Bringing these two approaches together provides for an interpretable, quantitative tool for assessing driving behavior across a wide range of driving scenarios. Beyond being interpretable, our approach relies on a relatively small set of basic assumptions that are easy to enumerate and reason about. Furthermore, an interpretable reachability model paired with kinematic assumptions provides a way to bound uncertainty about road users' reasonably foreseeable future locations. We demonstrate the applicability of the FSM to different driving scenarios and discuss the strengths and weaknesses of the model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：安全运动域：利用可达性分析在安全行驶领域中操作化可达性
我们提出了安全运动域（FSM），这是一种定量安全模型，用于判断驾驶员在任何时刻是否维持着无碰撞的逃生路线（即“出路”），该模型通过考虑驾驶员的生理能力及道路使用者可预见的行为来实现。安全行驶域（FST）提供了一个框架，用于表示驾驶员可获取的感官信息和可执行的动作。然而，自其近90年前首次发表以来，FST一直停留在概念层面，尚未有具体的计算实现。与此同时，可达性分析为评估道路使用者可能采取的动作提供了定量基础，使用可解释的运动学模型，但可达性模型迄今主要局限于工程与机器人学文献。将这两种方法结合，可提供一种可解释、定量的工具，用于评估各种驾驶场景下的驾驶行为。除了可解释性，我们的方法仅依赖于一组相对简单的基本假设，易于列举和推理。此外，可解释的可达性模型与运动学假设相结合，可为道路使用者可预见的未来位置提供不确定性边界。我们展示了FSM在不同驾驶场景中的适用性，并讨论了该模型的优缺点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a quantitative, interpretable safety model for driving, this paper introduces the Field of Safe Motion (FSM), which operationalizes the concept of affordances in driving by determining whether a driver maintains a collision-free escape route using reachability analysis. The method combines the conceptual Field of Safe Travel framework with kinematic reachability models to compute feasible escape trajectories based on driver capabilities and foreseeable actions of other road users. Experimental results show the FSM effectively identifies safe and unsafe driving states across diverse scenarios, demonstrating its utility in assessing driver behavior while highlighting limitations in modeling complex, unpredictable interactions.
为解决驾驶安全评估缺乏量化、可解释模型的问题，本文提出“安全运动域”（FSM），将“安全行驶域”（FST）框架与可达性分析相结合，通过运动学模型计算驾驶员在物理能力与他人可预见行为约束下的无碰撞逃生路径，实现对驾驶安全状态的实时量化评估。实验结果表明，FSM在多种驾驶场景中有效识别安全与危险状态，但也暴露出在建模复杂、不可预测的人类行为时的局限性。
ConsisVLA-4D: Advancing Spatiotemporal Consistency in Efficient 3D-Perception and 4D-Reasoning for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Wei Li, Jizhihui Liu, Li Yixing, Junwen Tong, Rui Shao, Liqiang Nie
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-06T16:55:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:55:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026, Project Page: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/CVPR26-ConsisVLA-4D
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models primarily focus on mapping 2D observations to actions, but exhibit notable limitations in spatiotemporal perception and reasoning: 1) spatial representations often rely on additional sensors, introducing substantial computational overhead; 2) visual reasoning is typically limited to future-frame prediction, lacking alignment with the instruction-grounded scene and thus compromising spatiotemporal consistency. To address these challenges, we propose ConsisVLA-4D, a unified and efficient framework that enhances spatiotemporal consistency in 3D perception and 4D reasoning. Specifically, we design: 1) CV-Aligner, which ensures cross-view object semantic consistency by filtering instruction-relevant regions and aligning object identities across multiple viewpoints; 2) CO-Fuser, which guarantees cross-object spatial geometric consistency by eliminating spatial relation ambiguities between objects across views using compact latent representations. Building upon these, we introduce 3) CS-Thinker to achieve cross-scene spatiotemporal consistency as actions unfold. It learns implicit knowledge of local dynamics from object-semantic tokens of CV-Aligner and global depth from geometric tokens of CO-Fuser, thereby enhancing efficient visual reasoning under scene variations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, benefiting from its efficient spatiotemporal consistency design, ConsisVLA-4D achieves 21.6% and 41.5% performance improvements, along with 2.3-fold and 2.4-fold inference speedups compared to OpenVLA on the LIBERO benchmark and real-world platforms, respectively.ConsisVLA-4D is open-sourced and publicly available at
中文标题/摘要
标题：ConsisVLA-4D：推进机器人操作中高效三维感知与四维推理的时空一致性
当前视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型主要关注将二维观测映射到动作，但在时空感知与推理方面存在明显局限：1）空间表示常依赖额外传感器，引入大量计算开销；2）视觉推理通常仅限于未来帧预测，缺乏与指令引导场景的对齐，从而损害时空一致性。为解决这些问题，我们提出ConsisVLA-4D，一个统一高效的框架，提升三维感知与四维推理中的时空一致性。具体而言，我们设计：1）CV-Aligner，通过过滤指令相关区域并跨多视角对齐物体身份，确保跨视角物体语义一致性；2）CO-Fuser，通过使用紧凑的潜在表示消除跨视角物体间空间关系歧义，保证跨物体空间几何一致性。在此基础上，我们引入3）CS-Thinker，实现动作展开过程中的跨场景时空一致性。它从CV-Aligner的物体语义标记中学习局部动态隐式知识，从CO-Fuser的几何标记中获取全局深度，从而在场景变化下增强高效视觉推理。大量实验表明，得益于其高效的时空一致性设计，ConsisVLA-4D在LIBERO基准和真实平台上的性能分别提升21.6%和41.5%，推理速度分别提升2.3倍和2.4倍。ConsisVLA-4D已开源，公开获取地址为：
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current Vision-Language-Action models in handling spatiotemporal consistency without extra sensors or future-frame prediction alone, ConsisVLA-4D introduces a unified framework with three core modules: CV-Aligner for cross-view semantic alignment, CO-Fuser for cross-object geometric consistency, and CS-Thinker for cross-scene spatiotemporal reasoning. These components jointly enable efficient 3D perception and 4D reasoning by leveraging compact latent representations and implicit dynamics learning. Experimental results on LIBERO and real-world platforms show 21.6% and 41.5% performance gains over OpenVLA, alongside 2.3x and 2.4x inference speedups.
为解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型在时空感知上的不足——如依赖额外传感器和缺乏指令对齐的视觉推理——本文提出ConsisVLA-4D框架，通过三个核心模块提升3D感知与4D推理的时空一致性：CV-Aligner通过过滤指令相关区域并跨视角对齐物体语义实现跨视图一致性；CO-Fuser利用紧凑的潜在表示消除物体间空间关系歧义；CS-Thinker整合语义与几何令牌以建模局部动态和深度，实现跨场景时空一致性。实验表明，该框架在LIBERO与真实平台上的性能较OpenVLA分别提升21.6%和41.5%，推理速度提升2.3倍和2.4倍。
Seeing the Goal, Missing the Truth: Human Accountability for AI Bias
Authors: Sean Cao, Wei Jiang, Hui Xu
First: 2026-02-10T08:01:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:55:41+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This research explores how human-defined goals influence the behavior of Large Language Models (LLMs) through purpose-conditioned cognition. Using financial prediction tasks, we show that revealing the downstream use (e.g., predicting stock returns or earnings) of LLM outputs leads the LLM to generate biased sentiment and competition measures, even though these measures are intended to be downstream task-independent. Goal-aware prompting shifts these intermediate measures toward the disclosed downstream objective, producing in-sample overfitting. Specifically, purpose leakage improves performance on data prior to the LLM's knowledge cutoff, but provides no advantage after the cutoff. This bias is strong enough that regularization of prompt instructions cannot fully address this form of overfitting. We further show that the bias can arise from users' unintentional conversational context that hints at the purpose. Overall, we document that AI bias due to "seeing the goal" is not an algorithmic flaw, but stems from human accountability in research design.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how human-defined goals influence Large Language Models (LLMs) by revealing downstream tasks such as stock prediction, which causes LLMs to generate biased sentiment and competition measures—even when those measures are meant to be task-agnostic. The researchers found that goal-aware prompting shifts intermediate outputs toward the disclosed objective, leading to in-sample overfitting, especially on data before the LLM’s knowledge cutoff, with no benefit afterward. Regularization of prompts fails to mitigate this bias, and it can also emerge from unintentional conversational context. The findings suggest that this form of AI bias stems not from algorithmic flaws but from human accountability in research design.
本研究探讨人类设定的目标如何通过目的导向认知影响大语言模型（LLM）的行为，动机源于发现揭示下游任务（如金融预测）会诱导LLM输出偏见。实验采用金融预测任务，发现披露LLM输出用途（如预测股票收益）会导致模型生成偏倚的舆情与竞争指标，即使这些指标本应与下游任务无关。目的导向提示使中间输出向披露目标偏移，引发样本内过拟合，提升截止日期前数据表现但无益于截止后数据。该偏倚在正则化提示指令下仍无法完全消除，且可源于用户无意的对话上下文，表明该偏倚根源在于研究设计中的人类责任而非算法缺陷。
CLAMP: Contrastive Learning for 3D Multi-View Action-Conditioned Robotic Manipulation Pretraining
Authors: I-Chun Arthur Liu, Krzysztof Choromanski, Sandy Huang, Connor Schenck
Venue: RSS
First: 2026-01-31T23:32:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:54:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Robotics: Science and Systems (RSS) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Leveraging pre-trained 2D image representations in behavior cloning policies has achieved great success and has become a standard approach for robotic manipulation. However, such representations fail to capture the 3D spatial information about objects and scenes that is essential for precise manipulation. In this work, we introduce Contrastive Learning for 3D Multi-View Action-Conditioned Robotic Manipulation Pretraining (CLAMP), a novel 3D pre-training framework that utilizes point clouds and robot actions. From the merged point cloud computed from RGB-D images and camera extrinsics, we re-render multi-view four-channel image observations with depth and 3D coordinates, including dynamic wrist views, to provide clearer views of target objects for high-precision manipulation tasks. The pre-trained encoders learn to associate the 3D geometric and positional information of objects with robot action patterns via contrastive learning on large-scale simulated robot trajectories. During encoder pre-training, we pre-train a Diffusion Policy to initialize the policy weights for fine-tuning, which is essential for improving fine-tuning sample efficiency and performance. After pre-training, we fine-tune the policy on a limited amount of task demonstrations using the learned image and action representations. We demonstrate that this pre-training and fine-tuning design substantially improves learning efficiency and policy performance on unseen tasks. Furthermore, we show that CLAMP outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across six simulated tasks and five real-world tasks. The project website and videos can be found at https://clamp3d.github.io/CLAMP/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLAMP：面向3D多视角动作条件机器人操作的对比学习预训练
利用预训练的2D图像表示在行为克隆策略中已取得巨大成功，并成为机器人操作的标准方法。然而，此类表示无法捕捉物体和场景的3D空间信息，而这些信息对于精确操作至关重要。本文提出CLAMP（面向3D多视角动作条件机器人操作的对比学习预训练），一种新颖的3D预训练框架，利用点云和机器人动作。从RGB-D图像与相机外参融合计算的点云中，我们重新渲染包含深度和3D坐标的多视角四通道图像观测，包括动态手腕视角，以提供更清晰的目标物体视图，支持高精度操作任务。预训练编码器通过在大规模模拟机器人轨迹上进行对比学习，学习将物体的3D几何与位置信息与机器人动作模式关联。在编码器预训练阶段，我们预训练一个扩散策略以初始化策略权重，用于后续微调，这对于提升微调样本效率和性能至关重要。预训练后，我们使用学习到的图像和动作表示，在少量任务演示数据上微调策略。实验证明，该预训练与微调设计显著提升了在未见任务上的学习效率和策略性能。此外，我们在六个模拟任务和五个真实世界任务中展示了CLAMP优于当前最先进基线的方法。项目网站和视频可访问：https://clamp3d.github.io/CLAMP/。

Motivated by the limitation of 2D image representations in capturing 3D spatial details for robotic manipulation, this work introduces CLAMP, a 3D pre-training framework that uses point clouds and robot actions to learn geometric and positional object representations via contrastive learning. The method re-renders multi-view four-channel images from RGB-D data and camera extrinsics, including dynamic wrist views, to enhance object visibility. Joint Treatment Effect Estimation from Incomplete Healthcare Data: Temporal Causal Normalizing Flows with LLM-driven Evolutionary MNAR Imputation
Authors: Olivia Jullian Parra, Sara Zoccheddu, David Catalan Cerezo, Tom Forzy, Franziska Ulrich, William Sutcliffe, Jakob Martin Burgstaller, Oliver Senn, Patrick Owen, Nicola Serra
First: 2026-05-06T16:53:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:53:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Target trial emulation (TTE) enables causal questions to be studied with observational data when randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are infeasible. Yet treatment-effect methods often address causal estimation, missingness, and temporal structure separately, limiting their robustness in electronic health records (EHRs), where time-varying confounding and missing-not-at-random (MNAR) biomarkers can reach 50%--80%. We propose a two-stage pipeline for treatment effect estimation from incomplete longitudinal EHRs. First, CausalFlow-T, a directed acyclic graph (DAG)-constrained normalizing flow with long short-term memory (LSTM)-encoded patient history, performs exact invertible counterfactual inference, avoiding approximation errors from variational inference and separating confounding through explicit causal structure. Ablations on four synthetic and one semi-synthetic benchmark with known counterfactuals show that DAG constraints and exact inference address distinct failure modes: neither compensates for the other. Second, because CausalFlow-T requires completed inputs, we introduce an LLM-driven evolutionary imputer that proposes executable imputation operators rather than individual entries, and evaluate it with three large language model (LLM) backends, including two open-source models. Across 30%--80% MNAR missingness, this imputer achieves the best pooled rank over biomarker and causal metrics, leading in point-wise accuracy and temporal extrapolation while preserving average treatment effect (ATE) recovery as statistical baselines degrade. On Swiss primary-care EHRs from adults with type 2 diabetes initiating a GLP-1 receptor agonist or SGLT-2 inhibitor, the pipeline estimates a per-protocol weight-loss difference of -0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96] favoring GLP-1 receptor agonists, consistent with randomized evidence and obtained from realistically incomplete real-world EHRs.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to estimate treatment effects from incomplete, time-varying electronic health records where missingness is often not at random, the paper proposes a two-stage method combining causal inference with large language model-driven imputation. The first stage, CausalFlow-T, uses a DAG-constrained normalizing flow with LSTM-encoded patient history to perform exact counterfactual inference, avoiding variational approximation errors and explicitly modeling confounding. The second stage employs an LLM-driven evolutionary imputer that generates executable imputation operators to fill in missing data, evaluated across multiple LLM backends. Experiments on synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks show that DAG constraints and exact inference address distinct failure modes and that the imputer outperforms baselines in accuracy and temporal extrapolation while preserving ATE recovery. On real Swiss primary-care EHRs for type 2 diabetes patients, the pipeline estimated a -0.98 kg weight loss advantage for GLP-1 receptor agonists over SGLT-2 inhibitors, aligning with RCT findings despite realistic missing data.
为了解决电子健康记录中因缺失数据（尤其是非随机缺失）导致的治疗效应估计难题，该研究提出两阶段方法：第一阶段，CausalFlow-T 采用有因果图约束的归一化流与 LSTM 编码患者历史，实现精确反事实推断，避免变分近似误差并显式建模混杂因素；第二阶段，引入基于大语言模型的进化填充器，生成可执行的填充操作而非单个值，经多个 LLM 后端评估。实验表明，该方法在 30% 至 80% 非随机缺失率下，优于基线模型在点估计精度、时间外推和平均处理效应恢复方面，且在真实糖尿病患者数据中，GLP-1 受体激动剂较 SGLT-2 抑制剂平均减重 0.98kg，与随机对照试验结果一致。
Adaptive Policy Selection and Fine-Tuning under Interaction Budgets for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Alper Kamil Bozkurt, Xiaoan Xu, Shangtong Zhang, Miroslav Pajic, Yuichi Motai
First: 2026-05-06T16:51:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:51:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In offline-to-online reinforcement learning (O2O-RL), policies are first safely trained offline using previously collected datasets and then further fine-tuned for tasks via limited online interactions. In a typical O2O-RL pipeline, candidate policies trained with offline RL are evaluated via either off-policy evaluation (OPE) or online evaluation (OE). The policy with the highest estimated value is then deployed and continually fine-tuned. However, this setup has two main issues. First, OPE can be unreliable, making it risky to deploy a policy based solely on those estimates, whereas OE may identify a viable policy with substantial online interaction, which could have been used for fine-tuning. Second--and more importantly--it is also often not possible to determine a priori whether a pretrained policy will improve with post-deployment fine-tuning, especially in non-stationary environments. As a result, procedures committing to a single deployed policy are impractical in many real-world settings. Moreover, a naive remedy that exhaustively fine-tunes all candidates would violate interaction budget constraints and is likewise infeasible. In this paper, we propose a novel adaptive approach for policy selection and fine-tuning under online interaction budgets in O2O-RL. Following the standard pipeline, we first train a set of candidate policies with different offline RL algorithms and hyperparameters; we then perform OPE to obtain initial performance estimates. We next adaptively select and fine-tune the policies based on their predicted performance via an upper-confidence-bound approach thereby making efficient use of online interactions. We demonstrate that our approach improves upon O2O-RL baselines with various benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向离线到在线强化学习的交互预算下自适应策略选择与微调
在离线到在线强化学习（O2O-RL）中，策略首先通过先前收集的数据集安全地离线训练，然后通过有限的在线交互进一步微调以执行任务。在典型的O2O-RL流程中，使用离线强化学习训练的候选策略通过离策略评估（OPE）或在线评估（OE）进行评估，选择估计价值最高的策略部署并持续微调。然而，该设置存在两个主要问题：首先，OPE可能不可靠，仅依赖这些估计值部署策略风险较高；而OE可能识别出需要大量在线交互才能验证可行性的策略，这些交互本可用于微调。其次——且更为重要的是——通常无法预先确定预训练策略在部署后是否能通过微调获得改进，特别是在非平稳环境中。因此，在许多现实场景中，固定部署单一策略的做法不切实际。此外，盲目地对所有候选策略进行穷举微调将违反交互预算约束，同样不可行。本文提出了一种新颖的自适应方法，用于在O2O-RL中在在线交互预算下进行策略选择与微调。遵循标准流程，我们首先使用不同离线强化学习算法和超参数训练一组候选策略；然后进行OPE以获得初始性能估计；接着，我们基于预测性能通过上置信界方法自适应选择并微调策略，从而高效利用在线交互。我们在多个基准上证明，该方法优于O2O-RL基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of off-policy evaluation and the inefficiency of exhaustive fine-tuning under interaction budgets, this paper proposes an adaptive policy selection and fine-tuning method for offline-to-online reinforcement learning. It first trains multiple candidate policies offline using different algorithms and hyperparameters, then estimates their performance via off-policy evaluation. Based on these estimates, it adaptively selects policies for online fine-tuning using an upper-confidence-bound strategy to balance exploration and exploitation while respecting interaction constraints. Experimental results show that this approach consistently outperforms standard baselines across multiple benchmarks by more efficiently allocating online interactions to promising policies.
为了解决离线到在线强化学习中离线评估不可靠和盲目细调浪费交互预算的问题，本文提出了一种基于置信上界策略的自适应政策选择与细调方法。首先使用不同算法和超参数训练多个候选策略，通过离线评估获得初步性能估计；随后根据预测性能自适应选择策略进行在线细调，以高效利用有限交互资源。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试中显著优于现有基线方法，能更有效地分配在线交互以提升最终性能。
Conditional outlier detection for clinical alerting
Authors: Milos Hauskrecht, Michal Valko, Shyam Visweswaran, Iyad Batal, Gilles Clermont, Gregory Cooper
First: 2026-05-06T16:51:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:51:44+00:00
Comments: AMIA 2010 Annual Symposium proceedings, pp. 286-290. Homer R. Warner Best Paper Award
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop and evaluate a data-driven approach for detecting unusual (anomalous) patient-management actions using past patient cases stored in an electronic health record (EHR) system. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to past patients may be due to a potential error and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. We evaluate this hypothesis using data obtained from the electronic health records of 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients. We base the evaluation on the opinions of a panel of experts. The results support that anomaly-based alerting can have reasonably low false alert rates and that stronger anomalies are correlated with higher alert rates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于条件的临床异常检测
我们开发并评估了一种基于数据的方法，用于利用电子健康记录（EHR）系统中存储的过往患者案例，检测不寻常（异常）的患者管理行为。我们的假设是，相对于过往患者而言不寻常的患者管理行为可能源于潜在错误，因此当遇到此类情况时值得发出警报。我们使用4,486例心脏手术后患者的电子健康记录数据对该假设进行评估，评估依据为专家小组的意见。结果支持基于异常的警报可具有相对较低的误报率，且异常程度越强，警报率越高。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to identify potentially erroneous patient-management actions in clinical settings, this study proposes a data-driven method to detect anomalies in patient care based on historical electronic health record data. The approach evaluates whether current actions deviate unusually from past patterns for similar patients, and triggers alerts when such deviations are detected. Evaluated on 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients with expert validation, the method demonstrated low false alert rates and showed that more pronounced anomalies were associated with higher alert rates, supporting the feasibility of anomaly-based clinical alerting.
为在临床环境中识别潜在错误的患者管理行为，本研究提出一种基于历史电子健康记录数据的异常检测方法，通过对比当前操作与历史模式发现异常。基于4,486例心脏术后患者的记录数据，该方法评估异常操作是否应触发警报，并由专家小组验证其有效性。结果表明，基于异常的警报系统可保持较低的误报率，且异常程度越高，警报触发率越高，支持该方法在临床工作流中的可行性。
A Universal Large Language Model -- Drone Command and Control Interface
Authors: Javier N. Ramos-Silva, Peter J. Burke
First: 2026-01-21T21:37:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:49:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The use of artificial intelligence (AI) for drone control can have a transformative impact on drone capabilities, especially when real world information can be integrated with drone sensing, command, and control, part of a growing field of physical AI. Large language models (LLMs) can be advantageous if trained at scale on general knowledge, but especially and in particular when the training data includes information such as detailed map geography topology of the entire planet, as well as the ability to access real time situational data such as weather. However, challenges remain in the interface between drones and LLMs in general, with each application requiring a tedious, labor intensive effort to connect the LLM trained knowledge to drone command and control. Here, we solve that problem, using an interface strategy that is LLM agnostic and drone agnostic, providing the first universal, versatile, comprehensive and easy to use drone control interface. We do this using the new model context protocol (MCP) standard, an open standard that provides a universal way for AI systems to access external data, tools, and services. We develop and deploy a cloud based Linux machine hosting an MCP server that supports the Mavlink protocol, an ubiquitous drone control language used almost universally by millions of drones including Ardupilot and PX4 framework.We demonstrate flight control of a real unmanned aerial vehicle. In further testing, we demonstrate extensive flight planning and control capability in a simulated drone, integrated with a Google Maps MCP server for up to date, real time navigation information. This demonstrates a universal approach to integration of LLMs with drone command and control, a paradigm that leverages and exploits virtually all of modern AI industry with drone technology in an easy to use interface that translates natural language to drone control.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simplify and unify the integration of large language models (LLMs) with drone command and control systems, this work introduces a universal interface leveraging the Model Context Protocol (MCP) standard. The method involves deploying a cloud-based Linux server running an MCP-compatible server that bridges LLMs with drone control via the Mavlink protocol, enabling natural language commands to translate into flight actions. Experimental results show successful real-world drone flight control and extensive simulated flight planning using real-time geographic data from a Google Maps MCP server, demonstrating a scalable, LLM-agnostic, and drone-agnostic solution for AI-driven drone operations.
为简化大语言模型（LLM）与无人机控制系统的集成，本研究提出一种基于模型上下文协议（MCP）标准的通用接口。该方法通过部署支持Mavlink协议的云服务器，将自然语言指令转化为无人机控制信号，兼容主流无人机框架如Ardupilot和PX4。实验结果表明，该系统可实现真实无人机的飞行控制，并在模拟环境中结合实时地理数据完成复杂飞行规划，验证了其通用性、易用性及对LLM与无人机平台的兼容性。
Beyond Semantics: An Evidential Reasoning-Aware Multi-View Learning Framework for Trustworthy Mental Health Prediction
Authors: Yucheng Ruan, Ling Huang, Qika Lin, Kai He, Mengling Feng
First: 2026-05-06T16:49:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:49:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated mental health prediction using textual data has shown promising results with deep learning and large language models. However, deploying these models in high-stakes real-world settings remains challenging, as existing approaches largely rely on semantic representations and often produce overconfident predictions under ambiguous, noisy, or shifted data. Moreover, most methods lack reliable uncertainty estimation, undermining trust in risk-sensitive mental health applications. To address these limitations, we formulate the task as a multi-view learning problem that integrates semantic information from encoder-only models with higher-level reasoning information from decoder-only models, where reasoning-aware representations and uncertainty modeling are obtained in a trustworthy manner. To ensure reliable fusion, we adopt an evidential learning framework based on Subjective Logic to explicitly model uncertainty and introduce an evidential fusion strategy that balances complementary views while discounting unreliable evidence. Benchmarking on three real-world datasets, Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity, reports accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751, respectively, demonstrating its potential for reliable mental health prediction. Additional experiments on robustness to noise and case studies for interpretability confirm that our proposed framework not only improves predictive performance but also provides trustworthy uncertainty estimates and human-understandable reasoning signals, making it suitable for risk-sensitive applications in mental health assessment.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for trustworthy mental health prediction in high-stakes settings, this work proposes a multi-view learning framework that combines semantic representations from encoder-only models with reasoning signals from decoder-only models, using an evidential reasoning approach based on Subjective Logic to model uncertainty and fuse views reliably. The framework introduces an evidential fusion strategy that weights views according to their reliability, discounting noisy or ambiguous evidence. Experimental results on three real-world datasets—Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity—show accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751 respectively, while additional tests confirm improved robustness to noise and enhanced interpretability, supporting its suitability for risk-sensitive mental health applications.
为提升高风险场景下心理健康预测的可信度，该研究提出一种基于证据推理的多视角学习框架，融合编码器模型的语义信息与解码器模型的推理信号。框架采用主观逻辑建模不确定性，并通过证据融合策略平衡互补视角同时剔除不可靠证据。在Dreaddit、SDCNL和DepSeverity三个真实数据集上分别达到0.835、0.731和0.751的准确率，且在噪声鲁棒性和可解释性案例中表现优异，证明其在风险敏感型心理健康评估中的适用性。
Physiologically Grounded Driver Behavior Classification: SHAP-Driven Elite Feature Selection and Hybrid Gradient Boosting for Multimodal Physiological Signals
Authors: Sahar Askari, Mohammad Mahdi Mirza Ali Mohammadi, Fatemeh Ensafdoust, Amin Golnari, Saeid Sanei
First: 2026-05-06T16:48:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-06T16:48:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An interpretable and scalable framework for decoding driving behaviors from multimodal physiological signals is proposed in this study. We utilize multimodal physiological driving behavior large-scale dataset comprising synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG), electromyography (EMG), and galvanic skin response (GSR) signals. Our approach involves rigorous preprocessing followed by a domain-specific feature extraction pipeline targeting time-domain, frequency-domain, and derived physiological indices. To address high dimensionality, we employ SHAP-based elite feature selection, retaining the top 250 features to reduce computational overhead while preserving predictive power. Hyperparameter optimization for extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) and light gradient boosting machine (LightGBM) models is conducted using Bayesian optimization via Optuna. Finally, a weighted soft-voting ensemble is constructed to leverage the complementary strengths of both gradient boosting frameworks. The results demonstrate that the proposed ensemble achieves a test accuracy of 80.91% and a macro-F1 score of 0.79, significantly outperforming single-modality baselines and traditional machine learning models. Ablation studies confirm an 8% performance gain over the best single modality (EEG), validating the necessity of multimodal fusion. SHAP analysis further validates the physiological plausibility of the model, revealing that the EEG contributes the majority of predictive weight, GSR and EMG features provide critical discriminatory signals for high-arousal and motor-intensive maneuvers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于生理基础的驾驶员行为分类：基于SHAP的精英特征选择与混合梯度提升用于多模态生理信号
本研究提出了一种可解释且可扩展的框架，用于从多模态生理信号中解码驾驶行为。我们利用包含同步脑电图（EEG）、肌电图（EMG）和皮肤电反应（GSR）信号的大规模多模态生理驾驶行为数据集。方法包括严格的预处理，随后是针对时域、频域及衍生生理指标的领域特定特征提取流程。为应对高维问题，我们采用基于SHAP的精英特征选择，保留前250个特征以降低计算开销同时保持预测能力。通过Optuna进行贝叶斯优化，对极端梯度提升（XGBoost）和轻量级梯度提升机（LightGBM）模型进行超参数调优。最终构建加权软投票集成模型，以充分利用两种梯度提升框架的互补优势。结果表明，所提集成模型在测试集上准确率达80.91%，宏F1分数为0.79，显著优于单模态基线及传统机器学习模型。消融实验验证，相比最佳单模态（EEG）性能提升8%，证实多模态融合的必要性。SHAP分析进一步验证模型的生理合理性，显示EEG贡献主要预测权重，GSR和EMG特征为高唤醒度及高运动强度操作提供关键判别信号。

Models are optimized via Bayesian hyperparameter tuning using Optuna for XGBoost and LightGBM, then combined in a weighted soft-voting ensemble.
